<h1><a href='index.html'>The Long Discourses</a></h1>


<table class="trans">
  <th class='ch'>(二) 第一分 遊行經 第二初</th>
  <th class='en'>2. The Journey<sup id="ref1"><a href="#n1">1</a></th>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a4'>佛說長阿含經 卷第二</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading">Fascicle 2 of the <em>Long Discourses</em></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a6'>後秦弘始年 佛陀耶舍共竺佛念譯</td>
    <td class="taisho_byline">Translated by Buddhayaśas and Chu Fonian during the Hongshi era [of emperor Yaoxing] of the Latter Qin Dynasty (412-3 CE)</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a8'>如是我聞： 一時，佛在羅閱城、耆闍崛山中。 與大比丘眾千二百五十人俱。</td>
    <td id='p1'>1. Thus I have heard:<sup id="ref2"><a href="#n2">2</a></sup> One time, the Buddha was staying on Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa at Rājagṛha. He was accompanied by a great assembly of 1,250 monks.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a9'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>1. Varṣākāra’s Visit</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a9'>是時，摩竭王阿闍世欲伐跋祇。 王自念言： 「彼雖勇健人眾豪強，以我取彼，未足為難。」</td>
    <td id='p2'>2. It was then<sup id="ref3"><a href="#n3">3</a></sup> that the King of Magadha Ajātaśatru wanted to attack Vṛji.<sup id="ref4"><a href="#n4">4</a></sup> The king thought to himself, “Although their brave people are fierce, it wouldn’t be enough to make it difficult for me to capture that country.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a11'>時，阿闍世王命婆羅門大臣禹舍而告之曰： 「汝詣耆闍崛山至世尊所，持我名字，禮世尊足。 問訊世尊： 『起居輕利，遊步強耶？』 又白世尊： 『跋祇國人自恃、勇健，民眾豪強。 不順伏我，我欲伐之。 不審世尊何所誡勅？』 若有教誡，汝善憶念。 勿有遺漏。 如所聞說，如來所言，終不虛妄。」</td>
    <td id='p3'>3. King Ajātaśatru then summoned his brahmin prime minister Varṣākāra<sup id="ref5"><a href="#n5">5</a></sup> and told him, “You will visit the Bhagavān on Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa and bow at the Bhagavān’s feet in my name. Then, ask about the Bhagavān’s health: ‘Have you been getting around easily? Have you had the strength to travel?’ Also say this to the Bhagavān: ‘The people of Vṛji are independent and brave, and the population is fierce. They won’t submit to me, so I am going to attack them. What instruction might the Bhagavān have about that?’ If he has some instruction, then you are to remember it well. Don’t forget any of it. Report to me the Tathāgata’s words just as you heard them, for they are never false.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a18'>大臣禹舍受王教已，即乘寶車詣耆闍崛山。 到所止處，下車步進。 至世尊所，問訊畢，一面坐。 白世尊曰： 「摩竭王阿闍世𥡳首佛足，敬問慇懃： 『起居輕利？遊步強耶？』 又白世尊： 『跋祇國人自恃勇健，民眾豪強。 不順伏我，我欲伐之。 不審世尊何所誡勅？』」</td>
    <td id='p4'>4. Having accepted the king’s instructions, Prime Minister Varṣākāra rode a precious chariot to Mount Gṛdhrakūṭa. Reaching a place to stop, he dismounted and proceeded on foot. Once he reached the Bhagavān and had finished with exchanging greetings with him, Varṣākāra sat to one side. He then said to the Bhagavān, “The King of Magadha Ajātaśatru bows his head at the Buddha’s feet and respectfully and politely asks, ‘Have you been getting around easily? Have you had the strength to travel?’ He also has this to say to the Bhagavān: ‘The people of Vṛji are independent and brave, and the population is fierce. They won’t submit to me, so I am going to attack them. What instruction might the Bhagavān have about that?’”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>2. Seven Ways to Be Unconquerable</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a24'>爾時，阿難在世尊後執扇扇佛。 佛告阿難： 「汝聞跋祇國人數相集會，講議正事不？」</td>
    <td id='p5'>5. Ānanda was standing behind the Bhagavān holding a fan and fanning him at the time.<sup id="ref6"><a href="#n6">6</a></sup> The Buddha asked Ānanda, “Have you heard that the people of Vṛji frequently hold gatherings to discuss proper matters?”<sup id="ref7"><a href="#n7">7</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a26'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a26'>佛告阿難： 「若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增盛。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p6'>6. The Buddha said to Ānanda, “If they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a27'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人君臣和順？ 上下相敬不？」</td>
    <td id='p7'>7. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji and their rulers and ministers are in harmony? Do those in high and low stations respect each other?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a28'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11a29'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增盛。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p8'>8. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b1'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人奉法，曉忌，不違禮度不？」</td>
    <td id='p9'>9. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji accept the Dharma, understand what to avoid, and don’t go against the rules of propriety?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b2'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b2'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增盛。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p10'>10. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b4'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人孝事父母，敬順師長不？」</td>
    <td id='p11'>11. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji are dutiful to their parents and obey their teachers?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b5'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b5'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增上。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p12'>12. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more.<sup id="ref8"><a href="#n8">8</a></sup> Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b6'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人恭於宗廟，致敬鬼神不？」</td>
    <td id='p13'>13. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji venerate their ancestral shrines and pay their respects to the spirits and gods?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b7'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b8'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增上。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p14'>14. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b9'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人閨門真正、潔淨、無穢，至於戲笑，言不及邪，不？」</td>
    <td id='p15'>15. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji are honest, pure, and undefiled inside their households, and their words never become perverse even in jest?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b11'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b11'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增盛。 其國久安，無能侵損。</td>
    <td id='p16'>16. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b12'>「阿難，汝聞跋祇國人宗事沙門，敬持戒者，瞻視、護、養，未嘗懈惓不？」</td>
    <td id='p17'>17. “Ānanda, have you heard that the people of Vṛji traditionally serve ascetics, respect those who observe the precepts, and look up to, protect, and support them without ever tiring of it?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b14'>答曰： 「聞之。」</td>
    <td>He replied, “I’ve heard that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b14'>「阿難，若能爾者，長幼和順，轉更增盛。 其國久安，無能侵損。」</td>
    <td id='p18'>18. “Ānanda, if they can do that, then the old and the young will be in harmony, and they’ll prosper even more. Their country will be safe for a long time and impossible to conquer.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b15'>時，大臣禹舍白佛言： 「彼國人民，若行一法，猶不可圖。 況復具七？ 國事多故，今請辭還歸。」</td>
    <td id='p19'>19. Varṣākāra then said to the Buddha, “If the people of that country were to practice one of those things, it would be impossible to scheme against them. How would it be when they possess all seven? The duties of state are many, and I’d like to take my leave now.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b17'>佛言： 「可宜知是時。」</td>
    <td>The Buddha said, “You may go whenever you like.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b18'>時，禹舍即從座起，遶佛三匝，揖讓而退。</td>
    <td>Varṣākāra then rose from his seat, circled the Buddha three times, saluted him, and withdrew.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b19'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>3. Ensuring that the Teaching Won’t Decline</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b19'>其去未久，佛告阿難： 「汝勅羅閲祇，左右： 『諸比丘，盡集講堂。』」</td>
    <td id='p20'>20. Not long after he left, the Buddha told Ānanda, “Go order everyone to the left and right in Rājagṛha, ‘Monks, everyone is to assemble in the meeting hall!’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b20'>對曰： 「唯然。」 即詣羅閲祇城，集諸比丘，盡會講堂。 白世尊曰： 「諸比丘已集。 唯聖知時。」</td>
    <td id='p21'>21. Ānanda replied, “Very well.” He then went to the city of Rājagṛha and assembled all the monks together in the meeting hall. He then said to the Bhagavān, “The monks have assembled. The sage needs only to decide when to go.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b23'>爾時，世尊即從座起，詣法講堂，就座而坐。 告諸比丘： 「我當為汝說七不退法。 諦聽！諦聽，善思念之。」</td>
    <td id='p22'>22. The Bhagavān rose from his seat, went to the Dharma meeting hall, prepared a seat, and sat down. He then addressed the monks, “I will give a discourse on seven ways to not decline. Listen closely! Listen closely, and consider it well.”<sup id="ref9"><a href="#n9">9</a></sup></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b25'>時，諸比丘白佛言： 「唯然，世尊！願樂欲聞。」</td>
    <td>The monks said to the Buddha, “Very well, Bhagavān! We’d be happy to hear it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b26'>佛告諸比丘： 「七不退法者： 一曰、數相集會，講論正義，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p23'>23. The Buddha told the monks, “These are the seven ways to not decline: First, if we frequently hold meetings and discuss the proper meaning, then elders and juniors will be in accord, and the Dharma will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b28'>「二曰、上下和同，敬順，無違，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p24'>24. “Second, if those in high and low positions are in harmony, respectful, and don’t contradict each other, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the Dharma will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11b29'>「三曰、奉法，曉忌，不違制度，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p25'>25. “Third, if we uphold the Dharma, are clear about what to avoid, and don’t violate the rules, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the Dharma will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c1'>「四曰、若有比丘力能護眾，多諸知識，宜敬事之，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p26'>26. “Fourth, if the monks have the ability to protect the community, have many associates, and respect it as they should, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the teaching will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c3'>「五曰、念護心意，孝敬為首，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p27'>27. “Fifth, if we are careful in our thinking and dutiful to our leaders, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the teaching will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c4'>「六曰、淨修梵行，不隨欲態，則長幼和順，法不可壞。</td>
    <td id='p28'>28. “Sixth, if we purely cultivate the religious life and avoid situations of desire, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the teaching will be indestructible.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c5'>「七曰、先人後己，不貪名利，則長幼和順，法不可壞。」</td>
    <td id='p29'>29. “Seventh, if we put others first and ourselves after them and don’t covet fame or profit, elders and juniors will be in accord, and the teaching will be indestructible.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c6'>佛告比丘： 「復，有七法令法增長，無有損耗： 一者、樂於少事，不好多為，則法增長，無有損耗。 二者、樂於靜默，不好多言⋯ 三者、少於睡眠，無有昏昧⋯ 四者、不為群黨，言無益事⋯ 五者、不以無徳，而自稱譽⋯ 六者、不與惡人，而為伴黨⋯ 七者、樂於山林，閑靜獨處⋯ 如是，比丘則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p30'>30. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another seven things that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. [What are the seven?] First, if we enjoy few duties and don’t like doing many things, the teaching will grow and not diminish. Second, if we enjoy silence and don’t like a lot of talk … Third, if we’re seldom sleepy and don’t have any gloominess … Fourth, if we don’t keep company or talk about pointless subjects … Fifth, if we don’t praise ourselves for virtues we don’t have … Sixth, if we don’t associate with bad people or make them our companions … Seventh, if we’re happy living alone in quiet places in mountains and forests … In this way, the monks will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c13'>佛告比丘： 「復，有七法令法增長，無有損耗。 何謂為七？ 一者、有信，信於如來、至真、正覺，十號具足⋯ 二者、知慚，恥於己闕⋯ 三者、知愧，羞為惡行⋯ 四者、多聞，其所受持上中下善，義味、深奧，清淨、無穢，梵行具足⋯ 五者、精勤苦行，滅惡、修善，勤習不捨⋯ 六者、昔所學習，憶念、不忘⋯ 七者、修習智慧，知生滅法，趣賢聖要，盡諸苦本。 如是，七法則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p31'>31. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another seven things that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. What are the seven? First is having faith in the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who has fulfilled the ten epithets … Second is being conscientious and ashamed of one’s failings … Third is being modest and embarrassed by bad behavior … Fourth is being well-versed in and remembering what’s good in the beginning, middle, and end, that’s profound in content and expression, that’s pure and undefiled, and that perfects the religious life … Fifth is diligently practicing asceticism, ceasing to do what’s bad, cultivating what’s good, and not abandoning that effort … Sixth is remembering and not forgetting what’s been learned in the past … Seventh is cultivating wisdom, recognizing the law of arising and ceasing, heading for the noble goal, and ending the source of suffering. In this way, these seven things will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c21'>佛告比丘： 「復，有七法令法增長，無有損耗。 何謂為七？ 一者、敬佛。 二者、敬法。 三者、敬僧。 四者、敬戒。 五者、敬定。 六者、敬順父母。 七者、敬不放逸。 如是，七法則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p32'>32. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another seven things that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. What are the seven? First is respect for the Buddha. Second is respect for the Dharma. Third is respect for the Saṅgha. Fourth is respect for the precepts. Fifth if respect for <em>samādhi</em>. Sixth is respectful accord with one’s parents. Seventh is respect for carefulness. In this way, these seven things will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.11c25'>佛告比丘： 「復，有七法，則法增長，無有損耗。 何謂為七法？ 一者、觀身不淨。 二者、觀食不淨。 三者、不樂世間。 四者、常念死想。 五者、起無常想。 六者、無常苦想。 七者、苦無我想。 如是，七法則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p33'>33. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another seven things that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. What are the seven? First is contemplating the impurities of the body. Second is contemplating the impurities of food. Third is not being happy with the world. Fourth is always being mindful of the idea of death. Fifth is producing the idea of impermanence. Sixth is the idea that impermanence is painful. Seventh is the idea that what’s painful is not self. In this way, these seven things will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a1'>佛告比丘： 「復，有七法，則法增長，無有損耗。 何謂為七？ 一者、修念覺意，閑靜、無欲、出要無為。 二者、修法覺意⋯ 三者、修精進覺意⋯ 四者、修喜覺意⋯ 五者、修猗覺意⋯ 六者、修定覺意⋯ 七者、修護覺意⋯ 如是，七法則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p34'>34. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another seven things that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. What are the seven? First is cultivating the awakening factor of mindfulness with quietude, lack of desire, and the escape to the unconditioned. Second is cultivating the awakening factor of [analyzing] the teachings … Third is cultivating the awakening factor of effort … Fourth is cultivating the awakening factor of joy … Fifth is cultivating the awakening factor of calm … Sixth is cultivating the awakening factor of <em>samādhi</em> … Seventh is cultivating the awakening factor of equanimity … In this way, these seven things will grow and not diminish the teaching.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a6'>佛告比丘： 「有六不退法，令法增長，無有損耗。 何謂為六？ 一者、身常行慈，不害眾生。 二者、口宣仁慈，不演惡言。 三者、意念慈心，不懷壞損。 四者、得淨利養，與眾共之，平等無二。 五者、持賢聖戒，無有闕漏，亦無垢穢，必定不動。 六者、見賢聖道以盡苦際。 如是，六法則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p35'>35. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s six ways of not declining that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. What are the six? First is always acting with kindness and not harming sentient beings. Second is being benevolent when expressing oneself and not use harsh words. Third is being mindful, having kind thoughts, and not harboring harmful intent. Fourth is getting support in pure ways and sharing it with the community equitably. Fifth is observing the noble precepts without any omission and not having defilements so that one’s <em>samādhi</em> will surely be unperturbed. Sixth is seeing the noble path as the way to reach the end of suffering. In this way, these six things will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a12'>佛告比丘： 「復，有六不退法，令法增長，無有損耗。 一者、念佛。 二者、念法。 三者、念僧。 四者、念戒。 五者、念施。 六者、念天。 修此六念則法增長，無有損耗。」</td>
    <td id='p36'>36. The Buddha told the monks, “There’s another six ways of not declining that will make the teaching grow and not diminish. [What are the six?] First is recollecting the Buddha. Second is recollecting the Dharma. Third is recollecting the Saṅgha. Fourth is recollecting the precepts. Fifth is recollecting generosity. Sixth is recollecting the gods. Cultivating these six recollections will grow and not diminish the teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a17'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>3. At the Bamboo Park</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a17'>爾時，世尊於羅閲祇隨宜住已，告阿難言： 「汝等皆嚴。 吾欲詣竹園。」</td>
    <td id='p37'>37. After staying in Rājagṛha for as long as was fitting, the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “All of you, get ready. I’m going to visit the Bamboo Park.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a18'>對曰：「唯然，」即嚴衣鉢，與諸大眾侍從世尊。 路由摩竭，次到竹園。</td>
    <td id='p38'>38. Ānanda replied, “Very well,” and then prepared his robe and bowl and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Magadha and arrived next at the Bamboo Park.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a20'>往堂上坐。 與諸比丘說戒定慧： 「修戒獲定得大果報。 修定獲智得大果報。 修智心淨得等解脫。 盡於三漏，欲漏、有漏、無明漏，已得解脫，生解脫智： 『生死已盡，梵行已立，所作已辦。 不受後有。』」</td>
    <td id='p39'>39. The Buddha went up into the hall there and sat down. He gave the monks a discourse on the precepts, <em>samādhi</em>, and wisdom: “Cultivating precepts and obtaining <em>samādhi</em> wins a great reward. Cultivating <em>samādhi</em> and obtaining wisdom wins a great reward. Cultivating wisdom and purifying the mind wins complete liberation. With the end of the three contaminants, which are the contaminants of desire, existence, and ignorance, this knowledge of liberation arises after one is liberated: ‘My births and deaths have been ended, the religious life has been established, and the task has been accomplished. I won’t be subject to a later existence.’”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>4. Teaching at Pāṭaliputra</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a24'>爾時，世尊於竹園隨宜住已，告阿難曰： 「汝等皆嚴。 當詣巴陵弗城。」</td>
    <td id='p40'>40. After staying at the Bamboo Park for as long as was fitting, the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “All of you, get ready. I’m going to visit Pāṭaliputra.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a26'>對曰：「唯然。」 即嚴衣鉢，與諸大眾侍從世尊。 路由摩竭，次到巴陵弗城。 巴陵樹下坐。</td>
    <td id='p41'>41. Ānanda replied, “Very well.” He prepared his robe and bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Magadha and arrived next at the city Pāṭaliputra. They sat down under the patala trees there.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12a28'>時，諸清信士聞佛與諸大眾遠來至此，巴陵樹下。 即共出城，遙見世尊在巴陵樹下。 容貎端正，諸根寂定。 善調第一。 譬猶大龍以水清澄，無有塵垢，三十二相八十種好莊嚴其身。 見已歡喜，漸到佛所。 頭面禮足，却坐一面。</td>
    <td id='p42'>42. The believers there heard that the Buddha and the great assembly had arrived from far away and were sitting under the patala trees. They came out of the city and spotted the Bhagavān under the trees. He looked handsome and dignified with peaceful and settled faculties. He was the most well-behaved [person they’d seen]. Like a great elephant cleansed of dirt and dust with water, he was adorned with the thirty-two signs and eighty excellent features. They rejoiced upon seeing him and made their way to the Buddha. They then bowed their heads at his feet and withdrew to sit to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b4'>爾時，世尊漸為說法，示、教、利、喜。 諸清信士聞佛說法，即白佛言： 「我欲歸依佛、法、聖眾。 唯願世尊哀愍，聽許為優婆塞。 自今已後。不殺，不盜，不婬，不欺，不飮酒。 奉戒，不忘。 明，欲設供。 唯願世尊與諸大眾垂愍，屈顧。」</td>
    <td id='p43'>43. The Bhagavān then gradually taught them the Dharma by teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting them. Those believers who listened to the Buddha teach the Dharma said, “I would like to take refuge in the Buddha, Dharma, and noble Saṅgha. Please let the Bhagavān have compassion and permit us to become laymen. From now on, we won’t kill, steal, engage in sex, lie, or drink alcohol. We’ll uphold the precepts and won’t forget them. We’d also like to provide you with support tomorrow. Please let the Bhagavān and his great assembly have pity and allow us to care for them.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b9'>爾時，世尊默然許可。 諸清信士見佛默然，即從座起，遶佛三匝，作禮而歸。 尋為如來起大堂舍，平治處所。 掃、灑、燒香、嚴敷寶座。 供設既辦，往白世尊： 「所設已具。 唯聖知時。」</td>
    <td id='p44'>44. The Bhagavān then silently gave his consent. Seeing that the Buddha had remained silent, those believers rose from their seats, circled the Buddha three times, bowed, and returned home. They quickly set up a large residence hall for the Tathāgata and arranged the area. They swept, cleaned, burned incense, and prepared valuable seats. Having arranged everything, they returned and said to the Bhagavān, “We’ve arranged everything. It’s up to the noble ones to decide when to go.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b13'>於是，世尊即從座起，著衣，持鉢，與大眾倶詣彼講堂。 澡手，洗足，處中而坐。 時，諸比丘在左面坐，諸清信士在右面坐。</td>
    <td id='p45'>45. Thereupon, the Bhagavān rose from his seat, put on his robe, picked up his bowl, and went to that meeting hall with the great assembly. They washed their hands and feet, then they went into the building and sat down. The monks then sat facing to the left, and the believers sat facing to the right.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b15'>爾時，世尊告諸清信士曰： 「凡人犯戒，有五衰耗。 何謂為五？ 一者、求財所願不遂。 二者、設有所得日當衰耗。 三者、在所至處，眾所不敬。 四者、醜名惡聲流聞天下。 五者、身壞命終，當入地獄。」</td>
    <td id='p46'>46. The Bhagavān then addressed the believers, “Ordinary people who break the precepts will decline in five ways. What are the five? First, they won’t get the wealth that they want. Second, what they’ve managed to acquire will decline daily. Third, the community where they go to live won’t respect them. Fourth, a bad reputation and insults about them will be heard everywhere. Fifth, they’ll go to hell when their bodies break up and their lives end.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b20'>又告諸清信士： 「凡人持戒，有五功徳。 何謂為五？ 一者、諸有所求，輒得如願。 二者、所有財産增益，無損。 三者、所往之處，眾人敬愛。 四者、好名善譽周聞天下。 五者、身壞命終，必生天上。」</td>
    <td id='p47'>47. He also told the believers, “Ordinary people who observe the precepts have five virtues. What are the five? First, they easily acquire the things that they seek as they wish. Second, their property increases and doesn’t diminish. Third, people respect and like them wherever they go. Fourth, a good reputation and compliments about them are heard everywhere. Fifth, they’ll be born up in heaven when their bodies break up and their lives end.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b24'>時夜已半，告諸信士： 「宜各還歸。」 諸清信士即承佛教，遶佛三匝，禮足而歸。</td>
    <td id='p48'>48. Halfway through the night, he told the believers, “You should go home now.” Accepting the Buddha’s instruction, the believers circled him three times and bowed at his feet before leaving.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b26'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>5. The Construction of Pāṭaliputra</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b26'>爾時，世尊於後夜、明相出時，至閑靜處。 天眼清徹見諸大天神各封宅地，中神、下神亦封宅地。 是時，世尊即還講堂，就座而坐。 世尊知時，故問阿難： 「誰造此巴陵弗城？」</td>
    <td id='p50'>50. When the night ended and the light of dawn appeared, the Bhagavān went to a quiet place. With his clear and penetrating heavenly eye, he saw great heavenly spirits each taking up residence on Earth, and he saw middling and lesser spirits taking up residence on Earth, too. The Bhagavān returned to the meeting hall, prepared a seat, and sat down. Though he knew the answer, he asked Ānanda, “Who is building this city Pāṭaliputra?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12b29'>阿難白佛： 「此是禹舍大臣所造，以防禦跋祇。」</td>
    <td id='p51'>51. Ānanda said to the Buddha, “The minister Varṣākāra is building it as a defense against the Vṛji.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c2'>佛告阿難： 「造此城者正得天意。 吾於後夜明相出時，至閑靜處。 以天眼見諸大神天各封宅地，中、下諸神亦封宅地。 阿難，當知諸大神天所封宅地，有人居者安樂、熾盛。 中神所封，中人所居，下神所封，下人所居。 功徳多少，各隨所止。</td>
    <td id='p52'>52. The Buddha told Ānanda, “The builders of this city have correctly ascertained the will of the gods. When the night had ended and the light of dawn appeared, I went to a quiet place. With the heavenly eye, I saw great heavenly spirits each taking up residence on Earth, and I saw middling and lesser spirits taking up residence on Earth, too. Ānanda, you should know that people living where great heavenly spirits take up residence on Earth will be happy and prosperous. Middling people will live where the middling spirits take up residence, and lesser people will live where the lesser spirits take up residence. Where they live will be according to whether their virtues are many or few.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c7'>「阿難，此處賢人所居，商賈所集。 國法真實，無有欺罔。 此城最勝，諸方所推，不可破壞。 此城久後若欲壞時。必以三事。 一者、大水。 二者、大火。 三者、中人與外人謀，乃壞此城。」</td>
    <td id='p53'>53. “Ānanda, noble people live here, and merchants gather here. The country’s laws are true, and there isn’t any fraud. This city is the greatest, while those declining in other regions can’t destroy it. It would require three conditions for it to be destroyed in the future: First is a great flood. Second is a great fire. Third, civilized and barbaric people scheming together could destroy this city.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c11'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>6. The Laity at Pāṭaliputra</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c11'>時，巴陵弗諸清信士通夜供辦。 時，到白佛： 「食具已辦。 唯聖知時。」 時，清信士即便施設手自斟酌，食訖行水。 別取小牀敷，在佛前坐。</td>
    <td id='p54'>54. Then, the believers of Pāṭaliputra had prepared offerings during the night. They went to the Buddha and said, “The meal is fully prepared. It’s up to the nobles to decide when to go.” The believers then served their meals with their own hands, and they cleaned up when the meals were finished. Afterward, they brought out small seats and sat in front of the Buddha.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c14'>爾時，世尊即示之曰： 「今，汝此處賢智所居。 多持戒者淨修梵行。 善神歡喜即為呪願。 可敬知敬，可事知事。 博施兼愛。 有慈愍心，諸天所稱。 常與善倶，不與惡會。」</td>
    <td id='p55'>55. The Bhagavān then instructed them: “Today, you have venerable sages living here. Many people observe precepts and purely cultivate the religious life. Good spirits are delighted and chant prayers for them. They know to respect what’s respectable and serve who should be served. They’re liberal in generosity and affectionate to all. Their compassionate hearts are commended by the gods. They always associate with good people and don’t associate with evil people.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c18'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>7. Crossing the River</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c18'>爾時，世尊為說法已，即從座起，大眾圍遶侍送而還。 大臣禹舍從佛後行。時，作是念： 「今沙門瞿曇出此城門，即名此門為瞿曇門。 又，觀如來所渡河處，即名此處為瞿曇河。」</td>
    <td id='p56'>56. After he gave this teaching, the Bhagavān rose from his seat and returned to the grove with the great assembly surrounding him. While he followed behind the Buddha, Varṣākāra thought, “Now, the gate through which the ascetic Gautama leaves the city shall be named Gautama’s Gate. Also, the place where he crosses the river will be named Gautama’s Ford.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c22'>爾時，世尊出巴陵弗城，至于水邊。 時，水岸上人民眾多，中有乘船渡者。 或有乘筏，或有乘桴而渡河者。 爾時，世尊與諸大眾，譬如力士屈伸臂頃，忽至彼岸。</td>
    <td id='p57'>57. The Bhagavān then left the city Pāṭaliputra and continued to the river’s bank. A large crowd of people was on the shore, and some were being ferried across the river. Some rode on boats and some on rafts to cross the river. In the time it takes a strong man to flex his arm, the Bhagavān and his great assembly instantly crossed to the other shore.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.12c26'>世尊觀此義已，即說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p58'>58. After contemplating the meaning of this, the Bhagavān spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.12c27'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「佛為海船師；<br/>
    法橋渡河津。<br/>
    大乘道之輿，<br/>
    一切渡天人。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The Buddha is the ship captain on the sea;<br/>
    Dharma is a bridge across the river.<br/>
    Riding a carriage on the great roadway,<br/>
    All the gods and people can cross over.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.12c29'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>亦為自解結，<br/>
    渡岸得昇仙。<br/>
    都使諸弟子<br/>
    縛解得涅槃。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Indeed, by freeing himself of bondage,<br/>
    He crossed to the shore and became a sage.<br/>
    He helps all his disciples free themselves<br/>
    From bondage and to attain nirvāṇa.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a2'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>8. At Kuṭigrāmaka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a2'>爾時，世尊從跋祇遊行，至拘利村。 在一林下，告諸比丘： 「有四深法： 一曰、聖戒。 二曰、聖定。 三曰、聖慧。 四曰、聖解脫。 此法微妙、難可解知。 我及汝等不曉了故，久在生死，流轉無窮。」</td>
    <td id='p60'>60. The Bhagavān then toured Vṛji until he reached Kuṭigrāma. While they stayed in a grove there, he addressed the monks, “There are four profound principles: First are noble precepts. Second is noble <em>samādhi</em>. Third is noble wisdom. Fourth is noble liberation. These principles are sublime and difficult to understand. Because you and I didn’t comprehend them, we’ve been in birth and death for a long time, cycling around endlessly.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a6'>爾時，世尊觀此義已，即說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p61'>61. After contemplating the meaning of this, the Bhagavān spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.13a7'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「戒定慧解上<br/>
    唯佛能分別。<br/>
    離苦而化彼，<br/>
    令斷生死習。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“High precepts, <em>samādhi</em>, wisdom, and freedom<br/>
    Are discernible only to a buddha.<br/>
    Free of suffering, he teaches others<br/>
    To end the arising of birth and death.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a9'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>9. Teaching the Dharma Mirror At Nādikā</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a9'>爾時，世尊於拘利村隨宜住已，告阿難： 「倶詣那陀村。」 阿難受教，即著衣，持鉢，與大眾倶侍從世尊。 路由跋祇，到那陀村，止揵椎處。</td>
    <td id='p62'>62. After he had stayed in Kuṭigrāmaka for as long as was fitting, the Bhagavān told Ānanda, “Let’s go visit Nādikā.” Accepting this instruction, Ānanda put on his robe, took his bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Vṛji to Nādikā and stopped at Kuñjikā’s home when they arrived.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a12'>爾時，阿難在閑靜處，默自思惟： 「此那陀村，十二居士： 一、名伽伽羅，二、名伽陵伽，三、名毘伽陀，四、名伽利輸，五、名遮樓，六、名婆耶樓，七、名婆頭樓，八、名藪婆頭樓，九、名陀梨舍㝹，十、名藪達利舍㝹，十一、名耶輸，十二、名耶輸多樓。 此諸人等，今者命終，為生何處？ 復，有五十人命終。 又復，有五百人命終。 斯生何處？」</td>
    <td id='p63'>63. Then, Ānanda was in a quiet place and silently thought to himself, “Here in Nādikā, there are twelve laymen: Their names are Karkaṭaka, Kaḍaṅgara, Nikaṭa, Kātyarṣabha, Cāru, Upacāru, Bhadra, Subhadra, *Darśana, *Sudarśana, Yaśas, and Yaśottara. Where will these men be born now that their lives have ended? There’s another fifty people whose lives have ended. And there’s yet another 500 people whose lives have ended. Where will they be born?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a19'>作是念已，從靜處起，至世尊所。 頭面禮足，在一面坐。 白佛言： 「世尊，我向靜處，默自思惟： 『此那陀村，十二居士，伽伽羅等，命終。 復，有五十人命終。 又，有五百人命終。 斯生何處？』 唯願，解說。」</td>
    <td id='p64'>64. After thinking this, he emerged from his quiet place and went to the Buddha. After bowing his head at the Buddha’s feet, he sat to one side. He said, “Bhagavān, I was in a quiet place and silently thought to myself, ‘There are twelve laymen here in Nādikā, Karkaṭaka and others, whose lives have ended. There’s another fifty people whose lives have ended. And there’s yet another 500 people whose lives have ended. Where were they be born?’ Please explain this.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a23'>佛告阿難： 「伽伽羅等，十二人，斷五下分結，命終生天。 於彼，即般涅槃不復還此。 五十人命終者斷除三結，婬、怒、癡薄。 得斯陀含，還來此世，盡於苦本。 五百人命終者斷除三結，得須陀洹。 不墮惡趣，必定成道。 往來七生，盡於苦際。</td>
    <td id='p65'>65. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Those twelve people, Karkaṭaka and the others, had cut the five lower bonds and were born in heaven when their lives ended. There, they were completely extinguished and did not return to this world. Those fifty people whose lives have ended had eliminated the three bonds and weakened their lust, anger, and delusion. They became once-returners who’ll return to this world and then end the source of suffering. Those five hundred people whose lives have ended had eliminated the three bonds and become stream-enterers. They didn’t fall to bad destinies and will surely achieve awakening. They’ll be reborn seven times to reach the end of suffering.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13a29'>「阿難，夫生有死自世之常，此何足怪？ 若，一一人死，來問我者，非擾亂耶？」</td>
    <td id='p66'>66. “But, Ānanda, what’s strange about someone being born and dying in the normal course of life? Wouldn’t it be troublemsome if you came and asked me about each person who dies?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b1'>阿難答曰： 「信爾，世尊。 實是擾亂。」</td>
    <td id='p67'>67. Ānanda replied, “I believe it would be, Bhagavān. It really would be troublesome.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b2'>佛告阿難： 「今當為汝說於法鏡，使聖弟子知所生處： 『三惡道盡，得須陀洹，不過七生必盡苦際。』 亦能為他說如是事。</td>
    <td id='p68'>68. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Now, I’ll teach the Dharma mirror to you, which lets a noble disciple know about where they’ll be born: ‘The three bad destinies have ended, I’ve attained stream entry, and I’ll surely reach the end of suffering in no more than seven births.’ They can also explain such matters for other people.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b5'>「阿難，『法鏡』者謂聖弟子得不壞信。 歡喜信佛、如來、無所著、等正覺，十號具足。</td>
    <td id='p69'>69. “Ānanda, ‘Dharma mirror’ refers to a noble disciple attaining unwavering faith. They rejoice and believe in the Buddha, the Tathāgata, Arhat, and Completely Awakened One who possesses the ten epithets.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b7'>「歡喜信法真正、微妙、自恣所說無有時節、示涅槃道、智者所行。</td>
    <td id='p70'>70. “They rejoice and believe in the Dharma as genuine, sublime, freely taught at any time, showing the path to <em>nirvāṇa</em>, and practiced by the wise.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b8'>「歡喜信僧善共和同，所行質直，無有䛕諂，道果成就。 上下和順，法身具足。 向須陀洹，得須陀洹。 向斯陀含，得斯陀含。 向阿那含，得阿那含。 向阿羅漢，得阿羅漢。 四雙、八輩是謂如來賢聖之眾。 甚可恭敬世之福田。</td>
    <td id='p71'>71. “They rejoice and believe in the Saṅgha as being good together, harmonious, honest in conduct, without deception, and accomplished in the path’s rewards. Their low and high stations are in accord, and they possess the Dharma body. They head for stream entry and become stream-enterers. They head for once-returning and become once-returners. They head for non-returning and become non-returners. They head for becoming an arhat and become arhats. These four pairs and eight kinds of people are called the Tathāgata’s noble Saṅgha. They are the most respectable fields of merit in the world.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b13'>「信賢聖戒清淨、無穢、無有缺漏、明哲所行、獲三昧定。</td>
    <td id='p72'>72. “They also believe in the noble precepts as pure, undefiled, without defect or contamination, practiced by intelligent people, and obtaining <em>samādhi</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b14'>「阿難，是為法鏡。 使聖弟子知所生處： 『三惡道盡，得須陀洹，不過七生必盡苦際。』 亦能為他說如是事。」</td>
    <td id='p73'>73. “Ānanda, this is the Dharma mirror. It causes noble disciples to know this about where they’ll be born: ‘The three bad destinies have ended, I’ve attained stream entry, and I’ll surely reach the end of suffering in no more than seven births.’ They can also explain such matters for other people.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b17'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>10. Āmrapālī’s Invitation at Vaiśālī</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b17'>爾時，世尊隨宜住已，告阿難： 「倶詣毘舍離國。」 即受教行，著衣，持鉢，與大眾倶侍從世尊。 路由跋祇，到毘舍離，坐一樹下。</td>
    <td id='p74'>74. After the Bhagavān had stayed for as long as was fitting, he told Ānanda, “Let’s visit Vaiśālī.” Accepting this instruction, Ānanda put on his robe, took his bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Vṛji to Vaiśālī and sat down under a tree.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b19'>有一婬女名菴婆婆梨。 聞佛將諸弟子來至毘舍離，坐一樹下，即嚴駕寶車，欲往詣佛所，禮拜，供養。 未至之間，遙見世尊顏貎端正，諸根特異，相、好備足。 如星中月。 見已，歡喜，下車。 歩進漸至佛，所頭面禮足，却坐一面。</td>
    <td id='p75'>75. There was a prostitute named Āmrapālī. Hearing that the Buddha had led his disciples to Vaiśālī and was sitting under a tree, she prepared horses and a precious chariot to go honor and give offerings to him. Before she arrived, she saw from afar that the Bhagavān’s appearance was handsome, his faculties were exceptional, and he was replete with signs and excellencies. He looked like the moon among stars. Seeing this, she rejoiced and dismounted from her chariot. She made her way to the Buddha, bowed her head at his feet, and withdrew to sit to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b24'>爾時，世尊漸為說法，示、教、利、喜。 聞佛所說，發歡喜心，即白佛言： 「從今日始，歸依三尊。 唯願，聽許於正法中為優婆夷。 盡此形壽，不殺，不盜，不邪婬，不妄語，不飮酒。」</td>
    <td id='p76'>76. The Bhagavān then gradually taught her the Dharma by teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting her. After listening to the Buddha’s teaching, she rejoiced and said, “From this day forward, I take refuge in the three worthy things. Please permit me to become a laywoman in the correct teaching. For the rest of my life, I won’t kill, steal, commit sexual misconduct, speak falsely, or drink alcohol.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13b28'>又白佛言： 「唯願，世尊及諸弟子明受我請，即於今暮，止宿我園。」</td>
    <td id='p77'>77. She also said to the Buddha, “Please let the Bhagavān and his disciples clearly accept my invitation and stop for the night at my grove.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c1'>爾時，世尊默然受之。 女見佛默然許可，即從座起，頭面禮足，遶佛而歸。</td>
    <td id='p78'>78. The Bhagavān silently accepted her request. Seeing that he had accepted, she rose from her seat, bowed her head at his feet, circled the Buddha, and returned home.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c2'>其去未久，佛告阿難： 「當與汝等詣彼園觀。」</td>
    <td id='p79'>79. Not long after she left, the Buddha told Ānanda, “Let’s all go visit her park.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c3'>對曰：「唯然。」 佛即從座起，攝持衣鉢，與眾弟子千二百五十人倶，詣彼園。</td>
    <td id='p80'>80. Ānanda replied, “Very well.” The Buddha then rose from his seat, gathered his robes and bowl, and went to her park with the assembly of 1,250 disciples.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c5'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>11. The Licchavi Laymen Visit</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c5'>時，毘舍離諸隷車輩聞佛在菴婆婆梨園中止住。 即便嚴駕五色寶車。 或乘青車、青馬，衣、蓋、幢幡、官屬皆青。 五色車馬皆亦如是。</td>
    <td id='p81'>81. Then, a group of Licchavi men in Vaiśālī had heard that the Buddha was going to stop at Āmrapālī’s Park. They prepared five-colored precious chariots and horses. Some rode on blue chariots with blue horses, and their clothes, parasols, banners, and retainers were all blue. The horses and chariots in other four colors were likewise.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c8'>時，五百隷車，服色盡同，欲往詣佛。 菴婆婆梨辭佛還家，中路逢諸隷車。 時，車行𩣺疾，與彼寶車共相鈎撥。 損折幢蓋而不避道。 隷車責曰： 「汝恃何勢行不避道？ 衝撥我車損折麾蓋！」</td>
    <td id='p82'>82. 500 Licchavis dressed entirely in these colors decided to visit the Buddha. Āmrapālī was returning home after speaking with him and met those Licchavi chariots on the road. They were traveling very fast and collided with each other. Their banners and parasols were knocked down, but she didn’t move out of their path. The Licchavis yelled at her, “Who do you think you are, not yielding the road to us? You’ve run into our chariots and knocked down our banners and parasols!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c12'>報曰： 「諸貴，我已請佛明日，設食歸家供辦，是以行速。 無容相避。」</td>
    <td id='p83'>83. She replied, “Gentlemen, I’ve invited the Buddha for a meal tomorrow, and now I’m going home to prepare it, so I was going fast. There wasn’t enough room to avoid you.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c14'>諸隷車即語女曰： 「且置汝請，當先與我。 我當與汝百千兩金。」</td>
    <td id='p84'>84. The Licchavis said to her, “Perhaps you could set aside your invitation and let us go first? We’ll give you 100,000 ounces of gold!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c15'>女尋答曰： 「先請已定；不得相與。」</td>
    <td id='p85'>85. She quickly replied, “My invitation has already been settled; I can’t give it to you.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c16'>時，諸隷車又語女曰： 「我更與汝十六倍百千兩金。 必使我先。」</td>
    <td id='p86'>86. The Licchavis again said to her, “We’ll give you sixteen times 100,000 ounces of gold. Surely, it can be arranged for us to go first?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.3c17'>女猶不肯。 「我請已定。 不可爾也。」</td>
    <td id='p87'>87. She still didn’t agree. “My invitation has already been settled. I can’t do that!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c18'>時，諸隷車又語女曰： 「我今與爾中分國財。 可先與我。」</td>
    <td id='p88'>88. The Licchavis again said to her, “We’ll give you the middle part of the country! Couldn’t you let us go first?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c19'>女又報曰： 「設使擧國財寶我猶不取。 所以然者？ 佛住我園先受我請。 此事已了，終不相與。」</td>
    <td id='p89'>89. She also replied, “Even if it were all the wealth in the country, I still wouldn’t give it to you. Why is that? The Buddha is staying in my park, and he’s already accepted my invitation. The matter is settled, and I’d never give it to someone else.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c22'>諸隷車等各振手，歎咤： 「今，由斯女闕我初福。」 即便前進徑詣彼園。</td>
    <td id='p90'>90. The Licchavis each clapped their hands and sighed, “Now, my first bit of good fortune is lost because of this woman!” They went on ahead, then, to visit her park.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c23'>爾時，世尊遙見五百隷車，車馬數萬，填道而來。 告諸比丘： 「汝等欲知忉利諸天遊戲園觀威儀容飾，與此無異。</td>
    <td id='p91'>91. The Bhagavān saw those 500 Licchavi chariots from afar, their horses were so numerous, filling the road as they approached. He told the monks, “If you want to know how dignified-looking and decorated the Trāyastriṃśa gods are when they entertain themselves in their scenic parks, it’s no different than this.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.13c26'>「汝等比丘，當自攝心具諸威儀。 云何比丘自攝其心？ 於是，比丘內身身觀。 精勤、不懈，憶念、不忘，捨世貪、憂。 外身身觀。 精勤、不懈，憶念、不忘，捨世貪、憂。 內、外身觀。 精勤、不懈⋯捨世貪、憂。 受、意、法觀亦復如是。</td>
    <td id='p92'>92. “You monks, you should collect your thoughts and behave in a dignified manner. How does a monk collect his thoughts? Here, a monk observes body internally to be body. Diligent and unflagging, he’s mindful and doesn’t forget it, setting aside worldly greed and sorrow. He observes body externally to be body. Diligent and unflagging, he’s mindful and doesn’t forget it, setting aside worldly greed and sorrow. He observes body internally and externally to be body. Diligent and unflagging, [he’s mindful and doesn’t forget it,] setting aside worldly greed and sorrow. He also observes feelings, mind, and principles in the same way.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a1'>「云何比丘具諸威儀？ 於是，比丘可行知行，可止知止。 左右顧視，屈伸，俯仰，攝持衣鉢。 食飮湯藥，不失宜。 則善設方便，除去蔭蓋，行、住、坐、臥，覺、寤，語、默。 攝心、不亂。 是謂比丘具諸威儀。</td>
    <td id='p93'>93. “How does a monk behave in a dignified manner? Here, a monk knows to walk when he should walk and to stop when he should stop. He looks left and right before bending and stretching, looking down and up, or gathering his robes and bowl. When he eats, drinks, and takes medicine, he doesn’t forget what’s appropriate. He skillfully prepares when relieving himself, taking away screens, while walking, standing, sitting, and lying down, and while awake, asleep, speaking, and being quiet. He collects his thoughts and isn’t disturbed. This is a monk who behaves in a dignified manner.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a6'>爾時，五百隷車往至菴婆婆梨園，欲到佛所下車歩進。 頭面禮足却坐一面。 如來在座光相獨顯，蔽諸大眾譬如秋月。 又如天地清、明、淨、無塵翳，日在虚空光明獨照。</td>
    <td id='p94'>94. Those 500 Licchavis arrived at Āmrapālī’s Park to visit the Buddha and then dismounted and proceeded on foot. They bowed their heads at his feet and withdrew to sit to one side. The Tathāgata glowed with a singular light there on his seat, outshining the great assemblies like the autumn moon. He was also like the sun shining with a singular brilliance when the sky and earth are clear, bright, and pure, and there isn’t any dust to obscure it.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a9'>爾時，五百隷車圍遶侍坐，佛於眾中光相獨明。</td>
    <td id='p95'>95. At that point, the Buddha glowed with a singular light in the middle of the assembly with the 500 Licchavis sitting around him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a11'>是時，坐中有一梵志名曰并𩞚，即從座起，偏袒右臂，右膝著地。 叉手向佛，以偈讃曰：</td>
    <td id='p96'>96. There was an ascetic named Paiṅgika who rose from his seat, bared his right shoulder, and knelt on his right knee. He saluted the Buddha with his palms together and praised him in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14a13'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「摩竭、鴦伽王<br/>
    為快得善利。<br/>
    身被寶珠鎧，<br/>
    世尊出其土。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The kings of Magadha and Aṅga<br/>
    Are delighted to obtain good profits.<br/>
    They wear jeweled armor,<br/>
    And the Bhagavān arose in their land.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14a15'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>威徳動三千，<br/>
    名顯如雪山！<br/>
    如蓮花開敷<br/>
    香氣甚微妙。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>His majesty moves three thousand [worlds],<br/>
    His name plain as the Himalayas!<br/>
    He’s like a blooming lotus flower<br/>
    With perfume most sublime.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14a17'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>今覩佛光明，<br/>
    如日之初出。<br/>
    如月遊虚空<br/>
    無有諸雲翳。<br/>
    世尊亦如是：<br/>
    光照於世間。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Looking at the Buddha’s glow now,<br/>
    It’s like the sun at daybreak.<br/>
    He’s like the moon roaming the sky<br/>
    Without any clouds to obscure it.<br/>
    The Bhagavān is likewise:<br/>
    His light shines on the world.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14a20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>觀如來智慧<br/>
    猶闇覩錠鐐。<br/>
     施眾以明眼<br/>
    決了諸疑惑。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Observing the Tathāgata’s wisdom<br/>
    Is like seeing with a torch in the dark.<br/>
    He gives sentient beings the clear vision<br/>
    To settle their doubts and confusion.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a22'>時，五百隷車聞此偈已，復告并𩞚： 「汝可重說！」</td>
    <td id='p97'>97. After they heard these verses, the 500 Licchavis again told Paiṅgika, “You can say that again!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a23'>爾時，并𩞚即於佛前再三重說。 時，五百隷車聞重說偈已，各脫寶衣以施并𩞚，并𩞚即以寶衣奉上如來。 佛愍彼故即為納受。</td>
    <td id='p98'>98. Paiṅgika then repeated what he said three times. After listening to him repeat these verses, they each gave their precious clothing to Paiṅgika, and he then offered their clothing to the Tathāgata. The Buddha accepted it out of compassion for them.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14a26'>爾時，世尊告毘舍離諸隷車曰： 「世有五寶甚為難得。 何等為五？ 一者如來、至真出現於世甚為難得。 二者如來正法能演說者，此人難得。 三者如來演法能信解者，此人難得。 四者如來演法能成就者，此人難得。 五者嶮危救、厄知、反復者，此人難得。 是謂五寶為難得也。</td>
    <td id='p99'>99. The Bhagavān then told the Licchavis of Vaiśālī, “The world has five treasures that are to find. What are the five? First, the arising of a Tathāgata, a Realized One, in the world is hard to find. Second, a person who can be taught the correct teaching by a Tathāgata is hard to find. Third, a person who’ll believe the Tathāgata’s teaching is hard to find. Fourth, a person who can accomplish the Tathāgata’s teaching is hard to find. Fifth, a person who’s saved from disaster, recognizes the danger, and returns [the favor] is hard to find. These are five treasures that are hard to find.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b3'>時，五百隷車聞佛示、教、利、喜已，即白佛言： 「唯願世尊及諸弟子明受我請！」</td>
    <td id='p100'>100. When they heard the Buddha teach, instruct, profit, and delight them, those 500 Licchavis said to the Buddha, “Please let the Bhagavān and his disciples accept our invitation!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b5'>佛告隷車： 「卿已請我，我今便為得供養已，菴婆婆梨女先已請訖。」</td>
    <td id='p101'>101. The Buddha told the Licchavis, “Now that you have invited me, I’ll let you give me your offerings after I’m finished with Āmrapālī’s prior invitation.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b6'>時，五百隷車聞菴婆婆梨女已先請佛，各振手而言： 「吾欲供養如來。而今此女已奪我先！」 即從座起，頭面禮佛，遶佛三匝，各自還歸。</td>
    <td id='p102'>102. When they heard that Āmrapālī had invited the Buddha first, those 500 Licchavis snapped their fingers and said, “We wanted to give offerings to the Tathāgata, but this woman beat us to it!” They rose from their seats, bowed their heads to the Buddha, circled him three times, and returned to their homes.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b9'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>12. Āmrapālī’s Offerings</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b9'>時，菴婆婆梨女即於其夜種種供辦。 明日時到，世尊即與千二百五十比丘整衣、持鉢，前後圍遶。 詣彼請所就座而坐。</td>
    <td id='p103'>103. That night, Āmrapālī prepared a variety of offerings. When morning arrived, the Bhagavān went to the place of her invitation surrounded by 1,250 monks who had adjusted their robes and carried their bowls. They prepared seats and sat down.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b12'>時，菴婆婆梨女即設上饌供佛及僧。 食訖，去鉢并除机案。 時，女手執金瓶行澡水畢。 前白佛言： 「此毘耶離城所有園觀，我園最勝。 今，以此園貢上如來。 哀愍我故，願垂納受。」</td>
    <td id='p104'>104. Lady Āmrapālī then brought out fine dishes and offered them to the Buddha and the Saṅgha. When they were done with the meal, she took their bowls and cleared the table. She held a gold jar for them to wash up. When they were done, she went before the Buddha and said, “This city of Vaiśālī has scenic parks, but my park is the best of them. Now, I donate this park to the Tathāgata. Please let him accept this out of compassion for me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b16'>佛告女曰： 「汝可以此園施佛為首及招提僧。 所以然者？ 如來所有園、林、房、舍、衣、鉢六物。 正使諸魔、釋、梵、大神力天無有能堪受此供者。</td>
    <td id='p105'>105. The Buddha told her, “You may donate this park to the Buddha and the Saṅgha of the four directions. Why is that? The Tathāgata possesses these six things: parks, groves, quarters, buildings, robe, and bowl. Even Māra, Śakra, Brahmā, and other gods of great miraculous power are incapable of accepting these offerings.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b19'>時，女受教，即以此園施佛為首及招提僧。 佛愍彼故即為受之。</td>
    <td id='p106'>106. She accepted his instruction and donated the park to the Buddha and the Saṅgha of the four directions. The Buddha accepted it out of compassion for her.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b21'>而說偈言：</td>
    <td id='p107'>107. He then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14b22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>起塔、立精舍、<br/>
    園果施清涼。<br/>
    橋船以渡人，<br/>
    曠野施水草。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Building shrines, monasteries,<br/>
    And fruitful parks give refreshment.<br/>
    Bridges and boats ferry people,<br/>
    And open land provides water and grass.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14b24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>及以堂閣施<br/>
    其福日夜增。<br/>
    戒具清淨者<br/>
    彼必到善方。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Raised halls, too, are gifts<br/>
    With merits which increase daily.<br/>
    One who is disciplined and pure<br/>
    Will surely arrive in a good region.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b26'>時，菴婆婆梨女取一小牀，於佛前坐。 佛漸為說法，示、教、利、喜。 施論、戒論、生天之論、欲為大患穢汙不淨、上漏為礙。 出要為上。</td>
    <td id='p108'>108. Āmrapālī fetched a small seat and sat in front of the Buddha. He then gradually taught her the Dharma, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting her. He discussed generosity, precepts, how to be born in heaven, the great danger of desire that’s polluting and impure, and the obstacle of the higher contaminants. [He praised] escaping it as the [most subtle, pure, and] supreme thing.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14b28'>爾時，世尊知彼女意柔軟、和悦、蔭蓋、微薄、易可開化。 如諸佛法，即為彼女說苦聖諦⋯苦集⋯苦滅⋯苦出要諦。</td>
    <td id='p109'>109. The Bhagavān then knew that her mind was softened, joyous, shaded, light, and easily educated. As was the way of Buddhas, he then taught her the noble truth of suffering … suffering’s formation … suffering’s cessation, and the truth of suffering’s escape.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c2'>時菴婆婆梨女信心清淨。 譬如淨潔白氈易為受色，即於座上遠塵離垢，諸法法眼生。 見法，得法，決定正住。 不墮惡道，成就無畏。 而白佛言： 「我今歸依佛、歸依法、歸依僧。」 如是再三。</td>
    <td id='p110'>110. Āmrapālī’s belief was purified. Like a white cloth that readily accepts a dye, she became removed from dust and free of defilement right there on her seat, and the Dharma eye arose in her. She saw the Dharma, got the Dharma, and was certainly in the proper standpoint. She wouldn’t fall to unpleasant destinies and had achieved fearlessness. She said to the Buddha, “Now, I take refuge in the Buddha, take refuge in the Dharma, and take refuge in the Saṅgha.” She repeated this three times.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c6'>「唯願如來聽我於正法中為優婆夷。 自今已後盡壽不殺、不盜、不邪婬、不欺、不飮酒。」</td>
    <td id='p111'>111. “Please let the Tathāgata permit me to become a laywoman in the correct teaching. From now on, I won’t kill, steal, commit sexual misconduct, lie, or drink alcohol for the rest of my life.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c8'>時，彼女從佛受五戒已，捨本所習，穢垢消除。 即從座起，禮佛，而去。</td>
    <td id='p112'>112. Once she had received the five precepts from the Buddha, she discarded her previous lifestyle, and eliminated her defilements. She then rose from her seat, bowed to the Buddha, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c10'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>13. At Veṇugrāmaka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c10'>爾時，世尊於毘舍離隨宜住已。 告阿難言： 「汝等皆嚴。 吾欲詣竹林叢。」</td>
    <td id='p113'>113. The Bhagavān then stayed in Vaiśālī for as long as was appropriate. Then he told Ānanda, “All of you, get ready. I’m going to Veṇugrāmaka.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c11'>對曰：「唯然，」即嚴衣鉢，與大眾侍從世尊。 路由跋祇至彼竹林。</td>
    <td id='p114'>114. Ānanda replied, “Very well,” prepared his robe and bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Vṛji to Veṇugrāmaka.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c13'>時，有婆羅門名毘沙陀耶聞佛與諸大眾詣此竹林。 默自思念： 「此沙門瞿曇名徳流布。 聞於四方，十號具足。 於諸天釋、梵、魔，若魔、天、沙門、婆羅門中，自身作證。 為他說法，上、中、下言皆悉真正。 義味深奧，梵行具足。 如此真人宜往瞻覩！」</td>
    <td id='p115'>115. There was then a priest named Viśvadāya who heard that the Buddha and the great assembly was visiting Veṇugrāmaka. He thought to himself, “This ascetic Gautama’s reputation for virtue has gotten around. It’s heard in all directions that he’s been given the ten epithets. He’s self-realized among the gods such as Śakra and Brahmā, or Māra, spirits, ascetics, and priests. He teaches the Dharma to others that’s genuine in the beginning, middle, and end. It’s meaning and content is profound, and it perfects the religious life. It would be fitting to meet such a true man as this!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c18'>時，婆羅門出於竹叢，往詣世尊。 問訊訖，一面坐。 世尊漸為說法，示、教、利、喜。 婆羅門聞已，歡喜，即請世尊及諸大眾明日舍食。 時，佛默然受請。 婆羅門知已許可，即從座起，遶佛，而歸。 即於其夜，供設飲食。 明日時到，唯聖知時。</td>
    <td id='p116'>116. That priest then left Veṇugrāmaka and visited the Bhagavān. After they exchanged greetings, he sat to one side. The Bhagavān then gradually taught him the Dharma, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting him. After listening to him, the priest rejoiced and invited the Bhagavān and the great assembly for a meal at his home the next day. The Buddha silently accepted his invitation. Recognizing that he had agreed, the priest rose from his seat, circled the Buddha, and returned home. That night, the priest prepared offerings of food and drink. It was up to the noble ones to decide when to go the next morning.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c24'>爾時，世尊著衣、持鉢，大眾圍遶，往詣彼舍。 就座而坐。 時，婆羅門設種種甘饌供佛及僧。 食訖去鉢。 行澡水畢，取一小牀，於佛前坐。</td>
    <td id='p117'>117. The Bhagavān put on his robe and took his bowl to the priest’s home while surrounded by the great assembly. There, they prepared their seats and sat down. The priest then provided a variety of sweet dishes as offerings to the Buddha and Saṅgha. When they were done eating, he took their bowls. When they were finished washing up, the priest got a small seat and sat in front of the Buddha.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.14c27'>爾時，世尊為婆羅門而作頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p118'>118. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse for the priest:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.14c28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「若以飮食<br/>
    衣服臥具；<br/>
    施持戒人<br/>
    則獲大果。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Whether it’s food and drink<br/>
    Or clothes and sleeping arrangements;<br/>
    A gift to those observing precepts<br/>
    Will win a great reward.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.15a1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>此為真伴，<br/>
    終始相隨。<br/>
    所至到處<br/>
    如影隨形。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>It’ll be a true companion,<br/>
    Following a person from beginning to end.<br/>
    It’ll arrive at their destination<br/>
    Like a shadow follows one’s body.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.15a3'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>是故，種善<br/>
    為後世糧。<br/>
    福為根基，<br/>
    眾生以安。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Therefore, plant what’s good<br/>
    And you’ll reap its bounty in a later life.<br/>
    With the foundation of their merits,<br/>
    Sentient beings will find peace.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.15a5'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>福為天護，<br/>
    行不危嶮。<br/>
    生不遭難，<br/>
    死則上天。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>With merit as their heavenly protection,<br/>
    They won’t run into disaster.<br/>
    Their births won’t be difficult,<br/>
    And they’ll ascend to heaven when they die.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a7'>爾時，世尊為婆羅門說微妙法。 示、教、利、喜已，從座而去。</td>
    <td id='p119'>119. The Bhagavān then taught the subtle Dharma for the priest. After he was taught, instructed, profited, and delighted by it, he rose from his seat and departed.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>14. Dispersal of the Great Assembly</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a8'>于時，彼土穀貴飢饉，乞求難得。 佛告阿難： 「勅此國內現諸比丘盡集講堂。」</td>
    <td id='p120'>120. During that time, there was a crop failure and famine in the region, which made soliciting alms difficult. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Order all the monks present in this country to assemble in the meeting hall.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a10'>對曰：「唯然。」 即承教旨，宣令遠近，普集講堂。 是時，國內大眾皆集，阿難白佛言： 「大眾已集。 唯聖知時。」</td>
    <td id='p121'>121. Ānanda replied, “Very well.” Having received his instruction, he announced to those near and far to assemble at the meeting hall. Once the great assembly in that country had gathered, Ānanda told the Buddha, “The great assembly has gathered. It’s up to the noble one to decide when to go.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a12'>爾時，世尊即從座起，詣於講堂，就座而坐。 告諸比丘： 「此土飢饉乞求難得。 汝等宜各分部隨所知識，詣毘舍離及越祇國，於彼安居可以無乏。 吾獨與阿難於此安居。 所以然者？ 恐有短乏。」 是時諸比丘受教即行。 佛與阿難獨留。</td>
    <td id='p122'>122. The Bhagavān then rose from his seat and went to the meeting hall where he prepared a seat and sat down. He then told the monks, “There’s a famine in this region that’s making it difficult to solicit alms. It would be best for all of you to split into groups and visit people you know in Vaiśālī and Vṛji where there’s no shortage of food. I will stay here in this safe abode with Ānanda. Why is that? It’s dangerous when there’s such shortages.” The monks accepted his instruction and did so. The Buddha and Ānanda stayed there by themselves.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a17'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>15. The Buddha Becomes Ill</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a17'>於後夏安居中，佛身疾生，擧體皆痛。 佛自念言： 「我今疾生，擧身痛甚，而諸弟子悉皆不在。 若取涅槃則非我宜。 今當精勤，自力以留壽命。」</td>
    <td id='p123'>123. After the summer retreat, the Buddha became sick, and his whole body ached. The Buddha thought to himself, “Now, I’ve become sick, and my whole body is in pain, but none of my disciples are present. It wouldn’t be appropriate for me to obtain <em>nirvāṇa</em>. Now, I must make effort to extend my life with my own power.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a21'>爾時，世尊於靜室出，坐清涼處。 阿難見已，速疾往詣，而白佛言： 「今觀尊顏，疾如有損。」</td>
    <td id='p124'>124. The Bhagavān then emerged from his quiet abode and sat in an open place. When Ānanda saw him, he hurried over and said, “Now that I see the sage, his sickness has made him look worse!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a23'>阿難又言： 「世尊有疾，我心惶懼，憂結荒迷。 不識方面。 氣息未絶。 猶少醒悟默思： 『如來未即滅度。 世眼未滅。 大法未損。』 何故今者不有教令於眾弟子乎？」</td>
    <td id='p125'>125. Ānanda also said, “When the Bhagavān was sick, I felt trepidation, and the bond of sorrow depressed me. I didn’t know which way I was going. But I’m still breathing. I think to myself, ‘The Tathāgata isn’t completely extinguished yet. The world’s eye hasn’t ceased yet. The great teaching hasn’t declined yet.’ Does he not have instructions for the assembled monks now?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15a26'>佛告阿難： 「眾僧於我有所須耶？ 若有自言：『我持眾僧』、『我攝眾僧』，斯人於眾應有教命。 如來不言：『我持於眾』、『我攝於眾』。 豈當於眾有教令乎？</td>
    <td id='p126'>126. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Does the Saṅgha need something from me? If there were someone who said, ‘I maintain the Saṅgha’ or ‘I collect the Saṅgha,’ this person would have some instructions for the assembly. The Tathāgata doesn’t say, ‘I maintain the Saṅgha’ or ‘I collect the Saṅgha.’ Why must he have some instructions for the Saṅgha?</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b1'>「阿難，我所說法內外已訖，終不自稱所見通達。 吾已老矣年且八十。 譬如故車方便修治得有所至，吾身亦然。 以方便力得少留壽，自力精進忍此苦痛。 不念一切想，入無想定時，我身安隱、無有惱患。</td>
    <td id='p127'>127. “Ānanda, the Dharma that I’ve taught is complete inside and out, but I’ve never claimed to have mastered its view. I’m elderly, fully eighty years old now. Like an old chariot that can still reach a destination with skill and maintenance, my body is likewise. With skill, I have the power to extend my life for a while longer, but it takes strength and effort to tolerate these pains. It’s when I’m not mindful of any conceptions and enter the <em>samādhi</em> without conception that my body is peaceful and without any distress.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b5'>「是故，阿難，當自熾燃熾燃於法。 勿他熾燃。 當自歸依，歸依於法。 勿他歸依。 云何自熾燃，熾燃於法，勿他熾燃？ 當自歸依，歸依於法，勿他歸依？</td>
    <td id='p128'>128. “Therefore, Ānanda, you must light yourself and light the Dharma. Don’t light something else. You must be your own refuge and take refuge in the Dharma. Don’t take refuge in something else. How does one light themselves, light the Dharma, and not light something else? How does one take refuge in themselves, take refuge in the Dharma, and not take refuge in something else?</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b9'>「阿難，比丘觀內身。 精勤無懈，憶念不忘，除世貪憂。 觀外身⋯觀內外身⋯精勤不懈，憶念不忘，除世貪憂。 受、意、法觀亦復如是。 是謂，阿難，自熾燃，熾燃於法，勿他熾燃。 當自歸依，歸依於法，勿他歸依。」</td>
    <td id='p129'>129. “Ānanda, a monk observes body internally [to be body.] Diligent and unflagging, he’s mindful and doesn’t forget it, setting aside worldly greed and sorrow. He observes body externally … observes body internally and externally … Diligent and unflagging, he’s mindful and doesn’t forget it, setting aside worldly greed and sorrow. He also observes feelings, mind, and principles in the same way. That’s how, Ānanda, one lights themselves, lights the Dharma, and doesn’t light something else. One must take refuge in themselves, take refuge in the Dharma, and not take refuge in something else.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b13'>佛告阿難： 「吾滅度後，能有修行此法者。 則為真我弟子，第一學者。」</td>
    <td id='p130'>130. The Buddha told Ānanda, “After my extinguishment, some will be capable of practicing this teaching. They will truly be my disciples and the best students.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b16'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>16. At Cāpāla Shrine</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b16'>佛告阿難： 「倶至遮婆羅塔。」</td>
    <td id='p131'>131. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Let’s us go to Cāpāla Shrine.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b16'>對曰：「唯然。」</td>
    <td id='p132'>132. “Very well.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b16'>如來即起，著衣，持鉢，詣一樹下。 告阿難： 「敷座。 吾患背痛。 欲於此止。」</td>
    <td id='p133'>133. The Tathāgata then rose, put on his robe, took his bowl, and went to a tree there. He told Ānanda, “Prepare a seat. My back pain is troubling me. I want to stop here.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b18'>對曰：「唯然。」 尋即敷座。</td>
    <td id='p134'>134. “Very well.” Ānanda quickly prepared his seat.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b19'>如來坐已，阿難敷一小座於佛前坐。 佛告阿難： 「諸有修四神足。 多修習行，常念不忘，在意所欲，可得不死一劫有餘。 阿難，佛四神足已多修行，專念不忘。 在意所欲，如來可止一劫有餘為世除冥，多所饒益，天人獲安。」</td>
    <td id='p135'>135. After the Tathāgata was seated, Ānanda prepared a small seat and sat in front of the Buddha. The Buddha told Ānanda, “There’s the cultivation of four miraculous abilities. Someone who often cultivates and trains in them, who’s always mindful and doesn’t forget them, can live for an eon or more if they so desire. Ānanda, the Buddha has often cultivated these four miraculous abilities, being mindful and not forgetting them. The Tathāgata can remain for an eon or more if he so desires to eliminate the darkness of the world, for the gain of many, and to give peace to gods and humans.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b24'>爾時，阿難默然不對。 如是再三，又亦默然。 是時，阿難為魔所蔽，曚曚不悟。 佛三現相，而不知請。</td>
    <td id='p136'>136. Ānanda was silent and didn’t reply. The Buddha repeated this three times, but Ānanda was silent. Under the influence of Māra, Ānanda had become drowsy and wasn’t alert. Three times the Buddha gave this clear hint, but he didn’t realize his request.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b26'>佛告阿難： 「宜知是時。」</td>
    <td id='p137'>137. The Buddha told Ānanda, “I’ll let you to decide when to go.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b26'>阿難承佛意旨，即從座起，禮佛，而去。 去佛不遠，在一樹下，靜意思惟。</td>
    <td id='p138'>138. Understanding what the Buddha meant, Ānanda rose from his seat, bowed to the Buddha, and departed. He wasn’t far away when the Buddha quieted his mind and contemplated there under the tree.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b28'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>17. Māra’s Request</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15b28'>其間未久，時，魔波旬來白佛： 「佛意無欲。 可般涅槃。 今正是時。 宜速滅度。」</td>
    <td id='p140'>140. It wasn’t long before Māra the Wicked One came to the Buddha and said, “The Buddha’s heart desires nothing. He can parinirvāṇa. Now would be a good time. He ought to be extinguished soon.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c1'>佛告波旬： 「且止！且止！我自知時。 如來今者未取涅槃。 須我諸比丘集，又能自調，勇捍無怯，到安隱處。 逮得己利，為人導師，演布經教，顯於句義。 若有異論，能以正法而降伏之。 又以神變，自身作證。 如是弟子皆悉未集。 又諸比丘尼、優婆塞、優婆夷，普皆如是，亦復未集。 今者要當廣於梵行，演布覺意，使諸天人普見神變。」</td>
    <td id='p141'>141. The Buddha told the Wicked One, “Stop! Stop! I’ll decide when it’s time. For now, the Tathāgata hasn’t decided on <em>nirvāṇa</em> yet. I need to assemble monks who can discipline themselves, overcome agitation without fear, and arrive at the place of safety. They need to obtain their own reward, teach other people, disseminate the <em>sūtra</em> teachings, and make their words and meanings plain. They need to defeat whatever other teachings there may be with the correct Dharma. They’ll need to realize these miracles for themselves. Such disciples haven’t assembled yet. There also need to be nuns, laymen, and laywomen who are all like this, and they haven’t assembled yet, either. What’s essential now is to broadly lecture about the awakened heart in the religious life and let gods and humans all see these miracles.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c8'>時，魔波旬復白佛言： 「佛昔於鬱鞞羅尼連禪水邊，阿遊波尼俱律樹下初成正覺。 我時至世尊所，勸請如來可般涅槃： 『今正是時，宜速滅度。』</td>
    <td id='p142'>142. Māra the Wicked One again said to the Buddha, “Once, the Buddha was sitting under the goatherd’s nyagrodha tree on the bank of the Nairañjanā River in Uruvilvā when he first achieved the perfect awakening.<sup id="ref10"><a href="#n10">10</a></sup> I went to the Bhagavān then and asked the Tathāgata to parinirvāṇa. I said, ‘Now would be a good time! You ought to be extinguished soon!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c12'>爾時，如來即報我言： 『止！止！波旬！ 我自知時， 如來今者未取涅槃，須我諸弟子集。乃至天人見神變化。 乃取滅度。』 佛今弟子已集，乃至天人見神變化。 今正是時。 何不滅度？」</td>
    <td id='p143'>143. “The Tathāgata replied to me, ‘Stop, Wicked One! Stop! I’ll decide when it’s time. For now, the Tathāgata hasn’t decided on nirvāṇa because I need to gather my disciples … let gods and humans see these miracles. Then, I’ll choose extinguishment.’ Now, the Buddha has gathered his disciples … let gods and humans see these miracles. Now would be a good time. Why not extinguish yourself?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c16'>佛言： 「止！止！波旬！佛自知時。 不久住也。 是後三月，於本生處拘尸那竭娑羅園雙樹間，當取滅度。」</td>
    <td id='p144'>144. The Buddha said, “Stop, Wicked One! Stop! The Buddha will decide when it’s time. He won’t remain for very long. In three months’ time, I will choose extinguishment in my homeland of Kuśinagara between a pair of trees in a sal grove.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c18'>時，魔即念： 「佛不虛言，今必滅度。」 歡喜踊躍，忽然不現。</td>
    <td id='p145'>145. Māra then thought, “The Buddha doesn’t speak falsely. He surely will be extinguished now!” He rejoiced and celebrated, then he instantly disappeared.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c19'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>18. Eight Causes of Earthquakes</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c19'>魔去未久，佛即於遮婆羅塔，定意三昧，捨命住壽。 當此之時，地大震動，舉國人民莫不驚怖，衣毛為竪。 佛放大光。 徹照無窮。 幽冥之處，莫不蒙明，各得相見。</td>
    <td id='p146'>146. Not long after Māra had departed, the Buddha entered a <em>samādhi</em> of settled mind at the Cāpāla Shrine in which he discarded his remaining life. There was a great earthquake at that moment, and there was no one in the whole country who wasn’t startled and frightened by it, who didn’t get goosebumps. The Buddha then emitted a great radiance. There was nowhere that it didn’t illuminate. Even the darkest places were made bright, and the beings there saw each other.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c23'>爾時，世尊以偈頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p147'>147. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.15c24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「有無二行中，<br/>
    吾今捨有為。<br/>
    內專三昧定，<br/>
    如鳥出於卵。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The two formations, being and non-being,<br/>
    I reject those conditioned states now.<br/>
    Focused in <em>samādhi</em> and settled,<br/>
    I’m like a bird emerging from its egg.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c26'>爾時，賢者阿難心驚毛竪。 疾行詣佛，頭面禮足，却住一面。 白佛言： 「恠哉！世尊！地動乃爾，是何因緣？」</td>
    <td id='p148'>148. It was difficult for the venerable Ānanda to think, he was so startled and had goosebumps. He hurried to the Buddha, bowed his head at his feet, and withdrew to stand to one side. He said to the Buddha, “How strange, Bhagavān! What was the reason for that earthquake?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.15c28'>佛告阿難： 「凡世地動，有八因緣。 何等八？ 夫地在水上。 水止於風。 風止於空。 空中大風有時自起，則大水擾。 大水擾則普地動。 是為一也。</td>
    <td id='p149'>149. The Buddha told Ānanda, “An earthquake usually has eight causes. What are the eight? The earth rests on water. Where the water stops, there’s air. Where the air stops, there’s space. Sometimes a great gale rises by itself in that empty space, which create a large wave in the water. That large wave of water then makes the whole Earth quake. That’s one cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a2'>復次，阿難，有時得道比丘、比丘尼及大神尊天。 觀水性多，觀地性少。 欲自試力，則普地動。 是為二也。</td>
    <td id='p150'>150. “Furthermore, Ānanda, sometimes a monk, nun, great spirit, sage, or exalted god achieves awakening. They observe how much water there is and how little earth there is. Wanting to test their power, they make the whole earth quake. This is the second cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a4'>復次，阿難，若始菩薩從兜率天降神母胎，專念不亂，地為大動。 是為三也。</td>
    <td id='p151'>151. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a bodhisattva’s spirit first descends from the Tuṣita Heaven to his mother’s womb while focused and undisturbed, there’s a great earthquake. This is the third cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a6'>復次，阿難，菩薩始出母胎，從右脇生，專念不亂，則普地動。 是為四也。</td>
    <td id='p152'>152. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a bodhisattva first emerges from his mother’s womb, he’s born from her side while focused and undisturbed, and the whole earth quakes. This is the fourth cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a8'>復次，阿難，菩薩初成無上正覺，當於此時，地大震動。 是為五也。</td>
    <td id='p153'>153. “Furthermore, Ānanda, at the moment a bodhisattva first achieves the unsurpassed and perfect awakening, there’s a great earthquake. This is the fifth cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a9'>復次，阿難，佛初成道，轉無上法輪，魔、若魔、天、沙門、婆羅門、諸天、世人所不能轉，則普地動。 是為六也。</td>
    <td id='p154'>154. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a buddha first achieves awakening and turns the unsurpassed Dharma wheel, which can’t be turned by [gods such as Śakra, Brahmā,] or Māra, demons and spirits, ascetics and priests, or worldly people and gods, then the whole earth quakes. This is the sixth cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a11'>復次，阿難，佛教將畢，專念不亂，欲捨性命，則普地動。 是為七也。</td>
    <td id='p155'>155. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when a buddha’s teaching is nearing it’s end and he discards his life force while focused and undisturbed, the whole earth quakes. This is the seventh cause.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a13'>復次，阿難，如來於無餘涅槃界般涅槃時，地大振動。 是為八也。 以是八因緣，令地大動。」</td>
    <td id='p156'>156. “Furthermore, Ānanda, when the Tathāgata parinirvāṇa-s in the realm of nirvāṇa without remainder, there’s a great earthquake. This is the eighth cause. These are the eight reasons that lead to an earthquake.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16a15'>爾時，世尊即說偈言：</td>
    <td id='p157'>157. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「無上二足尊，<br/>
    照世大沙門。<br/>
    阿難請天師，<br/>
    地動何因緣？</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The unsurpassed sage of bipeds,<br/>
    He’s a great ascetic who lights up the world.<br/>
    Ānanda asked the teacher of gods,<br/>
    ‘What’s the reason for this earthquake?’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>如來演慈音<br/>
    聲如迦毘陵。<br/>
    我說汝等聽：<br/>
    地動之所由。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The Tathāgata explained it in a kind voice<br/>
    That sounded like a <em>kalaviṅka</em>.<br/>
    ‘I’ll tell you all, so listen:<br/>
    There are these causes of earthquakes.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>地因水而止。<br/>
    水因風而住。<br/>
    若虛空風起，<br/>
    則地為大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The earth depends on water, which remains.<br/>
    Water depends on air, which abides.<br/>
    If a wind rises in that space,<br/>
    Then the earth is shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>比丘比丘尼<br/>
    欲試神足力。<br/>
    山海百草木，<br/>
    大地皆震動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Sometimes, a monk or nun<br/>
    Wants to test their miraculous power.<br/>
    The mountains, oceans, grasses, and trees,<br/>
    The whole earth is shaken.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>釋梵諸尊天<br/>
    意欲動於地。<br/>
    山海諸鬼神<br/>
    大地為震動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Śakra, Brahmā, and the venerable gods<br/>
    Sometimes want to shake the earth.<br/>
    Demons and spirits of mountains and oceans<br/>
    Also make the earth quake.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>菩薩二足尊，<br/>
    百福相已具；<br/>
    始入母胎時，<br/>
    地則為大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>When bodhisattvas and sages of bipeds,<br/>
    Replete with a hundred signs of merit,<br/>
    First enter their mother’s womb,<br/>
    Then the earth is shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16a28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>十月處母胎<br/>
    如龍臥茵蓐。<br/>
    初從右脇生，<br/>
    時地則大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>They dwell in the womb for ten months<br/>
    Like nāgas lying on a mat.<br/>
    When they first emerge from her right side,<br/>
    Then the earth is shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16b1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>佛為童子時，<br/>
    消滅使緣縛，<br/>
    成道勝無量。<br/>
    地則為大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>When the Buddha was a young man,<br/>
    He eliminated the conditions that fettered him<br/>
    And achieved awakening supreme and measureless.<br/>
    Then the earth was shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16b3'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>昇仙轉法輪<br/>
    於鹿野苑中。<br/>
    道力降伏魔，<br/>
    則地大為動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Become a sage, he turned the Dharma wheel<br/>
    There in the deer preserve.<br/>
    When he defeated Māra with awakening’s strength,<br/>
