    Then the earth was shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16b5'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>天魔頻來請，<br/>
    勸佛般泥洹。<br/>
    佛為捨性命，<br/>
    地則為大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The god Māra came to press his request,<br/>
    Urging the Buddha to parinirvāṇa.<br/>
    When the Buddha discarded his life force,<br/>
    Then the earth was shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16b7'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>人尊大導師，<br/>
    神仙盡後有。<br/>
    難動而取滅，<br/>
    時地則大動。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The human worthy, the great teacher,<br/>
    The miraculous sage ended later existences.<br/>
    He was hard to move but chose to go out,<br/>
    And the earth was shaken greatly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16b9'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>淨眼說諸緣，<br/>
    地動八事動。<br/>
    有此亦有餘；<br/>
    時地皆震動。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The pure eye explained these reasons,<br/>
    These eight situations that cause earthquakes.<br/>
    When there’s this, there’s also that;<br/>
    These are the occasions of earthquakes.’”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b11'>佛說長阿含經卷第二</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b14'>佛說長阿含經卷第三</td>
    <td class="fasc_heading"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b16'>後秦 弘始年 佛陀耶舍共竺佛念譯</td>
    <td class="taisho_byline"></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b18'>佛告阿難： 「世有八眾。 何謂八？ 一曰剎利眾。 二曰婆羅門眾。 三曰居士眾。 四曰沙門眾。 五曰四天王眾。 六曰忉利天眾。 七曰魔眾。 八曰梵天眾。</td>
    <td id='p162'>162. The Buddha told Ānanda, “The world has eight assemblies. What are the eight? First is the warrior assembly. Second is the priestly assembly. Third is the householder assembly. Fourth is the ascetic assembly. Fifth is the assembly of the four god kings. Sixth is the assembly of the Trāyastriṃśa gods. Seventh is the Māra assembly. Eighth is the assembly of Brahma gods.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b21'>我自憶念： 『昔者，往來與剎利眾坐起言語。 不可稱數。 以精進定力，在所能現。 彼有好色，我色勝彼。 彼有妙聲，我聲勝彼。 彼辭我退，我不辭彼。 彼所能說，我亦能說。 彼所不能，我亦能說。』</td>
    <td id='p163'>163. “I remember this about myself: ‘Once, I was reborn with an assembly of warriors, sitting, rising, and speaking with them. I can’t say how many times. With effort and the power of <em>samādhi</em>, I was able to appear among them. They had excellent forms, and my form was better than theirs. They had wonderful voices, and my voice was better than theirs. They would concede to me and withdraw, but I didn’t concede to them. What they could explain, I could explain, too. I could also explain what they couldn’t.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b25'>阿難，我廣為說法，示教利喜已，即於彼沒。 彼不知我是天、是人？ 如是至梵天眾。 往返無數。 廣為說法，而莫知我誰？」</td>
    <td id='p164'>164. “Ānanda, I broadly taught the Dharma, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting them, and then I would disappear from that place. They didn’t know whether I was a god or a human. So it was up to the assembly of Brahma gods. I couldn’t count how many times I went there. I broadly taught them the Dharma, but they didn’t know what I was.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b28'>阿難白佛言： 「甚奇，世尊！ 未曾有也，乃能成就如是。」</td>
    <td id='p165'>165. Ānanda said to the Buddha, “That’s amazing, Bhagavān! It’s unprecedented to be able to achieve something like that.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16b29'>佛言： 「如是微妙希有之法，阿難。 甚奇，甚特，未曾有也。 唯有如來能成此法。」</td>
    <td id='p166'>166. The Buddha said, “This is something that’s sublime and extraordinary, Ānanda. It’s amazing, incredible, and unprecedented. There’s only the Tathāgata who could achieve this.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c2'>又告阿難： 「如來能知受起、住、滅，想起、住、滅，觀起、住、滅。 此乃如來甚奇甚特未曾有法。 汝當受持。」</td>
    <td id='p167'>167. He also told Ānanda, “The Tathāgata is able to know the arising, duration, and cessation of feelings, the arising, duration, and cessation of conceptions, and the arising, duration, and cessation of contemplations. This is how the Tathāgata is something amazing, incredible, and unprecedented. You should remember this.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c5'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>19. Informing the Monks of Parinirvāṇa</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c5'>爾時，世尊告阿難： 「俱詣香塔，」 在一樹下，敷座而坐。</td>
    <td id='p168'>168. The Bhagavān told Ānanda, “Let’s go to Fragrance Shrine.”<sup id="ref11"><a href="#n11">11</a></sup> … They prepared seats to sit under a tree there.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c6'>佛告阿難： 「香塔左右現諸比丘。 普勅令集講堂。」</td>
    <td id='p169'>169. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Here at Fragrance Shrine, monks are present left and right. Order all of them to gather in the meeting hall.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c7'>阿難受教，宣令普集。 阿難白佛： 「大眾已集。 唯聖知時。」</td>
    <td id='p170'>170. Ānanda accepted this instruction and made the announcement for everyone to gather. Ānanda then said to the Buddha, “The great assembly has gathered. It’s up to the noble one to decide when to go.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c8'>爾時，世尊即詣講堂，就座而坐， 告諸比丘： 「汝等當知我以此法自身作證，成最正覺， 謂：四念處、四意斷、四神足、四禪、五根、五力、七覺意、賢聖八道。</td>
    <td id='p171'>171. The Bhagavān then went to the meeting hall, prepared a seat, and sat down. He told the monks, “All of you should know this teaching that I’ve realized for myself and which achieved the supreme and perfect awakening. It’s the four abodes of mindfulness, four mental disciplines, four miraculous abilities, four dhyānas, five faculties, five powers, seven factors of awakening, and the noble eightfold path.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c11'>「汝等宜當於此法中和同敬順。 勿生諍訟，同一師受，同一水乳。 於我法中宜勤受學。 共相熾然，共相娛樂。</td>
    <td id='p172'>172. “All of you must be in harmony and respectful in this teaching. Don’t create conflicts; accept the same teacher like water and milk in the same pot. You ought to diligently accept and train in my teaching. Light each other, and entertain each other.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c14'>比丘當知我於此法自身作證，布現於彼。 謂《貫經》、《祇夜經》、《受記經》、《偈經》、《法句經》、《相應經》、《本緣經》、《天本經》、《廣經》、《未曾有經》、《證喻經》、《大教經》。 汝等當善受持，稱量分別，隨事修行。 所以者何？ 如來不久，是後三月當般泥洹。」</td>
    <td id='p173'>173. “Monks, you should know this teaching that I’ve realized and widely demonstrate it to others. It’s the sūtras, songs, assurances, verses, inspirations, past events, past births, histories, extensive sūtras, unprecedented things, parables, and explanations. All of you must well remember them, weigh and discern them, and cultivate the practice as they dictate. Why is that? Not long from now, the Tathāgata will parinirvāṇa in three months’ time.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c19'>諸比丘聞此語已，皆悉愕然，殞絕迷荒。 自投於地，舉聲大呼曰： 「一何駛哉！ 佛取滅度。 一何痛哉！ 世間眼滅。 我等於此，已為長衰。」 或有比丘悲泣躃踊，宛轉㘁咷。 不能自勝。 猶如斬蛇，宛轉迴遑，莫知所奉。</td>
    <td id='p174'>174. When the monks heard him say this, they were all bewildered, cut short, perplexed, and agitated. They fell to the ground, all of them crying loudly: “How could it be so soon? The Buddha has chosen to be extinguished! How could it be so painful? The world will go blind! We’ll be left here in decline for a long time!” Some of the monks cried and beat their breasts, some twisted and turned as they wailed. They couldn’t control themselves. They were like a cut snake that twists, turns, and writhes urgently, not knowing where to go.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c24'>佛告諸比丘曰： 「汝等且止，勿懷憂悲。 天地人物，無生不終。 欲使有為不變易者，無有是處。 我亦先說恩愛無常。 合會有離。 身非己有，命不久存。」</td>
    <td id='p175'>175. The Buddha told the monks, “All of you, stop! Don’t feel grief and sorrow. Among the people and things in heaven and earth, there’s nothing born that doesn’t die. The desire for this conditioned life to stop changing is impossible. I’ve taught you in the past that love and affection is impermanent, too. Things that come together also fall apart. Your body is not your own, and life doesn’t last long.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.16c27'>爾時，世尊以偈頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p176'>176. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.16c29'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「我今自在，<br/>
    到安隱處；<br/>
    和合大眾，<br/>
    為說此義。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Now, I am free,<br/>
    I’ve arrived at the place of safety.<br/>
    I brought together a great assembly<br/>
    And taught them this meaning.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.17a2'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>吾年老矣，<br/>
    餘命無幾；<br/>
    所作已辦，<br/>
    今當捨壽。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>I’ve become elderly,<br/>
    With nothing much left of my life.<br/>
    I’ve accomplished the task,<br/>
    So now I discard my life.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.17a4'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>念無放逸，<br/>
    比丘戒具；<br/>
    自攝定意，<br/>
    守護其心。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Mindful and without carelessness,<br/>
    A monk perfects the precepts.<br/>
    Collecting himself, his mind is settled,<br/>
    And he guards his thoughts.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.17a6'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>若於我法，<br/>
    無放逸者；<br/>
    能滅苦本，<br/>
    盡生老死。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>In this teaching of mine,<br/>
    A person isn’t careless.<br/>
    They can destroy the root of suffering<br/>
    And end birth, old age, and death.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a8'>又告比丘： 「吾今所以誡汝者何？ 天魔波旬向來請我： 『佛意無欲。 可般泥洹。 今正是時。 宜速滅度。』</td>
    <td id='p177'>177. He also told the monks, “Why have I admonished you in this way? The god Māra the Wicked One made this request of me: ‘The Buddha’s heart desires nothing. He can parinirvāṇa. Now would be a good time. He ought to be extinguished soon.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a10'>「我言： 『止！止！波旬！ 佛自知時⋯ 須我諸比丘集⋯乃至⋯ 諸天普見神變。』</td>
    <td id='p178'>178. “I said, ‘Stop, Wicked one! Stop! The Buddha will decide when it’s time … My monks need to be assembled … <em>up to</em> … let gods and men all see these miracles.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a11'>「波旬復言： 『佛昔於鬱鞞羅尼連禪河水邊，阿遊波尼俱律樹下初成佛道。 我時白佛： 『佛意無欲。 可般泥洹。 今正是時。 宜速滅度。』</td>
    <td id='p179'>179. “Māra the Wicked One again said, ‘Once, the Buddha was sitting under the goatherd’s nyagrodha tree on the bank of the Nairañjanā River in Uruvilvā when he first achieved the perfect awakening. I went to the Bhagavān then and said: ‘The Buddha’s mind is desireless. He can parinirvāṇa. Now would be a good time. You ought to be extinguished soon.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a14'>「爾時，如來即報我言： 『止！止！波旬！ 我自知時。 如來今者未取滅度，須我諸弟子集⋯乃至⋯ 天人見神變化。 乃取滅度。』 今者如來弟子已集⋯乃至⋯ 天人見神變化。 今正是時。 宜可滅度。』</td>
    <td id='p180'>180. “‘The Tathāgata replied to me, “Stop, Wicked One! Stop! I’ll decide when it’s time. For now, the Tathāgata hasn’t decided on nirvāṇa because I need to gather my disciples … let gods and humans see these miracles. Then, I’ll choose extinguishment.” Now, the Buddha has gathered his disciples … let gods and humans see these miracles. Now would be a good time. Why not extinguish yourself?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a18'>「我言： 『止！止！波旬！ 佛自知時。 不久住也。 是後三月當般涅槃⋯』</td>
    <td id='p181'>181. “I said, ‘Stop, Wicked One! Stop! The Buddha will decide when it’s time. He won’t remain for very long. In three months’ time, I will choose extinguishment …’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a20'>「時，魔即念： 『佛不虛言。 今必滅度。』 歡喜踊躍，忽然不現。</td>
    <td id='p182'>182. “Māra then thought, ‘The Buddha doesn’t speak falsely. He surely will be extinguished now!’ He rejoiced and celebrated, then he instantly disappeared.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a21'>「魔去未久，即於遮波羅塔，定意三昧，捨命住壽。 當此之時，地大震動，天人驚怖，衣毛為竪。 佛放大光。 徹照無窮。 幽冥之處，莫不蒙明，各得相見。</td>
    <td id='p183'>183. “Not long after Māra had departed, I entered a <em>samādhi</em> of settled mind at the Cāpāla Shrine in which I discarded my remaining life. There was a great earthquake at that moment that startled and frightened both gods and humans, giving them goosebumps. The Buddha then emitted a great radiance. There was nowhere that it didn’t illuminate. Even the darkest places were made bright, and the beings there saw each other.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a24'>「我時頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p184'>184. “I then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.17a25'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「『有無二行中，<br/>
    吾今捨有為。<br/>
    內專三昧定，<br/>
    如鳥出於卵。』」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“‘Being and non-being, those two formations,<br/>
    I reject those conditioned states now.<br/>
    Focused in <em>samādhi</em> and settled,<br/>
    I’m like a bird emerging from its egg.’”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a27'>爾時，賢者阿難即從座起，偏袒右肩，右膝著地，長跪叉手。 白佛言： 「唯願世尊留住一劫，勿取滅度，慈愍眾生，饒益天人。」</td>
    <td id='p185'>185. The venerable Ānanda then rose from his seat, bared his right shoulder, knelt on his right knee, and saluted the Buddha with his palms together. He said, “Please, let the Bhagavān stay for an eon! Choose not to be extinguished out of compassion for sentient beings and the benefit of gods and humans!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17a29'>爾時，世尊默然不對。 如是三請，佛告阿難： 「汝信如來正覺道不？」</td>
    <td id='p186'>186. The Bhagavān remained silent and didn’t respond. Ānanda repeated this request three times, and then the Buddha told him, “Don’t you believe in the Tathāgata’s path of perfect awakening?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b2'>對曰： 「唯然，實信。」</td>
    <td id='p187'>187. He replied, “Indeed, I really do believe it!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b2'>佛言： 「汝若信者，何故三來觸嬈我為？ 汝親從佛聞，親從佛受： 『諸有能修四神足。 多修習行，常念不忘，在意所欲，可得不死一劫有餘。 佛四神足已多習行，專念不忘。 在意所欲，可止不死一劫有餘，為世除冥，多所饒益，天人獲安。』</td>
    <td id='p188'>188. The Buddha said, “If you believe it, why have you harassed me three times? You personally heard and received this from the Buddha: ‘There’s the cultivation of four miraculous abilities. Often cultivating and training in them, always mindful and not forgetting them, one can live for an eon or more if they so desire. The Buddha has often cultivated these four miraculous abilities, and he was mindful and didn’t forget them. The Tathāgata can remain for an eon or more if he so desires to eliminate the darkness of the world, for the gain of many, and to give peace to gods and humans.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b8'>「爾時，何不重請，使不滅度？ 再聞尚可。 乃至三聞，猶不勸請留住一劫，一劫有餘，為世除冥，多所饒益，天人獲安。</td>
    <td id='p189'>189. “Why didn’t you make this solemn request for me not to be extinguished at that time? You didn’t just hear this once. You heard it three times, but you didn’t urge me to remain for an eon or more to eliminate the darkness of the world, for the gain of many, and give peace to gods and humans.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b10'>「今汝方言。 豈不愚耶？ 吾三現相，汝三默然。 汝於爾時，何不報我： 『如來可止一劫，一劫有餘，為世除冥，多所饒益。』</td>
    <td id='p190'>190. “It’s only now that you speak. Are you an idiot? I gave an obvious hint three times, and three times you were silent. Why didn’t you say at the time, ‘May the Tathāgata remain for an eon or more to eliminate the darkness of the world and benefit many!’?</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b13'>「且止，阿難。 吾已捨性命。 已棄已吐。 欲使如來自違言者，無有是處。 譬如豪貴長者，吐食於地。 寧當復有肯還取食不？」</td>
    <td id='p191'>191. “Just stop, Ānanda. I’ve discarded my life force. I’ve thrown it away and rejected it. It’s impossible for a Tathāgata to go back on his word. Suppose a wealthy nobleman spits food on the ground. Would he pick it up and put it back in his mouth?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b16'>對曰：「不也。」</td>
    <td id='p192'>192. “No.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b16'>「如來亦然。 已捨已吐。 豈當復自還食言乎？」</td>
    <td id='p193'>193. “The Tathāgata is likewise. He has discarded and rejected his life force. How could he put his words back into his mouth?”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b18'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>20. At Āmragrāmaka</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b18'>佛告阿難： 「俱詣菴婆羅村，」即嚴衣鉢，與諸大眾侍從世尊。 路由跋祇到菴婆羅村，在一山林。</td>
    <td id='p194'>194. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Let’s go to Āmragrāmaka,” and Ānanda prepared his robe and bowl and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Vṛji to Āmragrāmaka and stayed in a mountain forest there.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b20'>爾時，世尊為諸大眾說戒、定、慧。 修戒獲定，得大果報。 修定獲智，得大果報。 修智心淨，得等解脫。 盡於三漏，欲漏、有漏、無明漏，已得解脫，生解脫智： 『生死已盡，梵行已立，所作已辦。 不受後有。』</td>
    <td id='p195'>195. The Bhagavān then taught the great assembly about precepts, <em>samādhi</em>, and wisdom. “Cultivating precepts and obtaining <em>samādhi</em> wins a great reward. Cultivating <em>samādhi</em> and obtaining wisdom wins a great reward. Cultivating wisdom and purifying the mind wins complete liberation. With the end of the three contaminants, which are the contaminants of desire, existence, and ignorance, the knowledge of liberation arises after one is liberated: ‘My births and deaths have been ended, the religious life has been established, and the task has been accomplished. I won’t be subject to a later existence.’”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b24'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>21. At Bhoga</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b24'>爾時，爾時，世尊於菴婆羅村，隨宜住已，佛告阿難： 「汝等皆嚴！ 當詣瞻婆村、揵茶村、婆梨婆村及詣負彌城。」</td>
    <td id='p196'>196. After he had stayed at the town of Āmragrāmaka for as long as was appropriate, the Buddha told Ānanda, “All of you, get ready. I will be going to Jambugrāmaka, Gaṇḍagrāmaka, Hastigrāmaka (?), and then to the city Bhoga.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b27'>對曰：「唯然，」即嚴衣鉢，與諸大眾侍從世尊。 路由跋祇漸至他城，於負彌城北，止尸舍婆林。</td>
    <td id='p197'>197. Ānanda replied, “Very well,” prepared his robe and bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Vṛji and made their way to that other city, stopping in the rosewood grove to the north of Bhoga.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17b29'>佛告諸比丘： 「當與汝等說四大教法。 諦聽！諦聽，善思念之。」</td>
    <td id='p198'>198. The Buddha told the monks, “I will give you a discourse on the four great ways of teaching. Listen closely! Listen closely, and consider it well.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c2'>諸比丘言： 「唯然，世尊。 願樂欲聞。」</td>
    <td id='p199'>199. The monks said, “Very well, Bhagavān. We’d be glad to hear it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c2'>「何謂為四？ 若有比丘作如是言： 『諸賢，我於彼村、彼城、彼國，躬從佛聞，躬受是教。』 從其聞者，不應不信，亦不應毀。 當於諸經推其虛實。 依律、依法究其本末。</td>
    <td id='p200'>200. “What are the four? Suppose a monk makes this statement: ‘Gentlemen, I personally heard and received this teaching from the Buddha in that town, city, or country.’ Those who hear his claim shouldn’t disbelieve or criticize it. They must judge from the <em>sūtra</em>s whether it’s true or false. Rely on the Vinaya and the Dharma to investigate whether it’s fundamental or superficial.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c6'>若其所言非經、非律、非法，當語彼言：『佛不說此，汝謬受耶！所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相違。賢士！汝莫受持，莫為人說，當捐捨之。』</td>
    <td id='p201'>201. “If what he said isn’t in the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, or the Dharma, they should say this to him: ‘The Buddha didn’t say this, so you must be mistaken. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier contradicts the Dharma. Sir, you shouldn’t remember it or teach it to others. You should discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c10'>若其所言依經、依律、依法者，當語彼言：『汝所言是真佛所說，所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相應。賢士！汝當受持，廣為人說，慎勿捐捨。』此為第一大教法也。</td>
    <td id='p202'>202. “If what he said is based on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma, they should say to him, ‘What you say is really what the Buddha taught. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier matches the Dharma. Sir, you should remember it and teach it widely to others. Take care not to discard it.’ This is the first great way of teaching.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c14'>「復次，比丘作如是言：『我於彼村、彼城、彼國，和合眾僧、多聞耆舊，親從其聞，親受是法、是律、是教。』從其聞者，不應不信，亦不應毀，當於諸經推其虛實，依法、依律究其本末，</td>
    <td id='p203'>203. “Furthermore, suppose a monk makes this statement: ‘I personally heard and received this Dharma, Vinaya, and teaching from a unified Saṅgha or well-versed elder in that town, city, or country.’ Those who hear his claim shouldn’t disbelieve or criticize it. They must judge from the <em>sūtra</em>s whether it’s true or false. Rely on the Vinaya and the Dharma to investigate whether it’s fundamental or superficial.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c18'>若其所言非經、非律、非法者，當語彼言：『佛不說此，汝於彼眾謬聽受耶！所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相違。賢士！汝莫持此，莫為人說，當捐捨之。』</td>
    <td id='p204'>204. “If what he said isn’t in the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, or the Dharma, they should say this to him: ‘The Buddha didn’t say this, so that Saṅgha you’ve been listening to must be mistaken. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier contradicts the Dharma. Sir, you shouldn’t remember it or teach it to others. You should discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c22'>若其所言依經、依律、依法者，當語彼言：『汝所言是真佛所說，所以者何？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相應。賢士！汝當受持，廣為人說，慎勿捐捨。』此為第二大教法也。</td>
    <td id='p205'>205. “If what he said is based on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma, they should say to him, ‘What you say is really what the Buddha taught. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier matches the Dharma. Sir, you should remember it and teach it widely to others. Take care not to discard it.’ This is the second great way of teaching.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.17c26'>「復次，比丘作如是言：『我於彼村、彼城、彼國，眾多比丘持法、持律、持律儀者，親從其聞，親受是法、是律、是教。』從其聞者，不應不信，亦不應毀，當於諸經推其虛實，依法、依律究其本末。</td>
    <td id='p206'>206. “Furthermore, suppose a monk makes this statement: ‘I personally heard and received this Dharma, Vinaya, and teaching from a group of monks who maintain the Dharma, Vinaya, and observances in that town, city, or country.’ Those who hear his claim shouldn’t disbelieve or criticize it. They must judge from the <em>sūtra</em>s whether it’s true or false. Rely on the Vinaya and the Dharma to investigate whether it’s fundamental or superficial.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a1'>若其所言非經、非律、非法者，當語彼言：『佛不說此，汝於眾多比丘謬聽受耶！所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相違。賢士！汝莫受持，莫為人說，當捐捨之。』</td>
    <td id='p207'>207. “If what he said isn’t in the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, or the Dharma, they should say this to him: ‘The Buddha didn’t say this, so that group of monks you’ve been listening to must be mistaken. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier contradicts the Dharma. Sir, you shouldn’t remember it or teach it to others. You should discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a5'>若其所言依經、依律、依法者，當語彼言：『汝所言是真佛所說，所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相應。賢士！汝當受持，廣為人說，慎勿捐捨。』是為第三大教法也。</td>
    <td id='p208'>208. “If what he said is based on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma, they should say to him, ‘What you say is really what the Buddha taught. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier matches the Dharma. Sir, you should remember it and teach it widely to others. Take care not to discard it.’ This is the third great way of teaching.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a9'>「復次，比丘作如是言：『我於彼村、彼城、彼國，一比丘持法、持律、持律儀者，親從其聞，親受是法、是律、是教。』從其聞者，不應不信，亦不應毀，當於諸經推其虛實，依法、依律究其本末。</td>
    <td id='p209'>209. “Furthermore, suppose a monk makes this statement: ‘I personally heard and received this Dharma, Vinaya, and teaching from a monk who maintains the Dharma, Vinaya, and observances in that town, city, or country.’ Those who hear his claim shouldn’t disbelieve or criticize it. They must judge from the <em>sūtra</em>s whether it’s true or false. Rely on the Vinaya and the Dharma to investigate whether it’s fundamental or superficial.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.18a14'>若所言非經、非律、非法者，當語彼言： 『佛不說此，汝於一比丘所謬聽受耶！ 所以然者？ 我依諸經、依法、依律，汝先所言，與法相違。 賢士！汝莫受持，莫為人說。 當捐捨之。』</td>
    <td id='p210'>210. “If what he said isn’t in the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, or the Dharma, they should say this to him: ‘The Buddha didn’t say this, so that monk you’ve been listening to must be mistaken. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier contradicts the Dharma. Sir, you shouldn’t remember it or teach it to others. You should discard it.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a18'>若其所言依經、依律、依法者，當語彼言：『汝所言是真佛所說，所以然者？我依諸經、依律、依法，汝先所言，與法相應。賢士！當勤受持，廣為人說，慎勿捐捨。』是為第四大教法也。」</td>
    <td id='p211'>211. “If what he said is based on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma, they should say to him, ‘What you say is really what the Buddha taught. Why is that so? We rely on the <em>sūtra</em>s, the Vinaya, and the Dharma. What you said earlier matches the Dharma. Sir, you should remember it and teach it widely to others. Take care not to discard it.’ This is the fourth great way of teaching.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a23'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>22. At Pāpā</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a23'>爾時，世尊於負彌城隨宜住已，告賢者阿難 俱詣波婆城， 對曰：「唯然！」即嚴衣鉢，與諸大眾侍從世尊，路由末羅至波婆城闍頭園中。</td>
    <td id='p213'>213. The Bhagavān stayed in Bhoga for as long as was appropriate, then he told Venerable Ānanda, “Let’s go to the city of Pāpā.” Ānanda replied, “Very well,” prepared his robe and bowl, and followed the Bhagavān with the great assembly. They took the road from Malla to the city of Pāpā and stopped in Cunda’s Park.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a26'>時，有工師子，名曰周那，聞佛從彼末羅來至此城，即自嚴服，至世尊所，頭面禮足，在一面坐。</td>
    <td id='p214'>214. There was a smith there named Cunda. Hearing that the Buddha had arrived in the city from Malla, he got dressed up and went to the Bhagavān. He bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet and sat to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18a28'>時，佛漸為周那說法正化，示教利喜，周那聞佛說法，信心歡喜，即請世尊明日舍食。</td>
    <td id='p215'>215. The Bhagavān gradually taught and properly edified Cunda, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting him. Hearing the Buddha teach the Dharma, Cunda believed it and rejoiced. He then invited the Bhagavān to his home for a meal the next day.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18b1'>時，佛默然受請。周那知佛許可，即從座起，禮佛而歸。尋於其夜供設飯食，明日時到，唯聖知時。</td>
    <td id='p216'>216. When the Buddha silently accepted his invitation, Cunda knew that he had agreed. He rose from his seat, bowed to the Buddha, and returned home. He immediately prepared meals to offer them during the night. When the next morning came, it was up to the noble one to decide when to go.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18b3'>爾時，世尊法服持鉢，大眾圍遶，往詣其舍， 就座而坐。 是時，周那尋設飲食，供佛及僧， 別煮栴檀樹耳，世所奇珍，獨奉世尊。</td>
    <td id='p217'>217. The Bhagavān then put on his robes, took his bowl, and went to Cunda’s home while surrounded by the great assembly. Once there, they prepared seats and sat down. Cunda immediately offered them the meals that he had prepared to the Buddha and Saṅgha. He had specially cooked sandalwood tree ears, making them the most exquisite in the world, and offered the dish only to the Bhagavān.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18b6'>佛告周那： 「勿以此耳與諸比丘。」 周那受教，不敢輙與。 時，彼眾中有一長老比丘，晚暮出家， 於其座上以餘器取。</td>
    <td id='p218'>218. The Buddha told Cunda, “Don’t serve these ears to the monks.” Cunda accept this instruction and didn’t dare serve any of it to them. There was then an elderly monk in the assembly who had left home during his sunset years. He took the remainder of that dish from where he sat.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18b9'>爾時，周那見眾食訖，并除鉢器，行澡水畢，即於佛前以偈問曰：</td>
    <td id='p219'>219. Seeing that the assembly had finished eating, Cunda took their bowls and washed them. When this was done, he went before the Buddha and asked a question in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b11'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「敢問大聖智，<br/>
    正覺二足尊，<br/>
    善御上調伏，<br/>
    世有幾沙門？」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“I would venture to ask the great sage,<br/>
    The perfectly awakened sage of bipeds,<br/>
    The skilled trainer, and supreme tamer:<br/>
    How many ascetics are there in the world?”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18b13'>爾時，世尊以偈答曰：</td>
    <td id='p220'>220. The Bhagavān answered him in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b14'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>如汝所問者，<br/>
    沙門凡有四；<br/>
    志趣各不同，<br/>
    汝當識別之。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“To answer your question,<br/>
    There are four kinds of ascetic in all.<br/>
    Each has different inclinations;<br/>
    You should know and discern them.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>一行道殊勝，<br/>
    二善說道義，<br/>
    三依道生活，<br/>
    四為道作穢。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The first practices the most supreme path.<br/>
    The second skillfully teaches the path’s meaning.<br/>
    The third gets their livelihood from the path.<br/>
    The fourth is defiled while on the path.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>何謂道殊勝？<br/>
    善說於道義，<br/>
    依道而生活，<br/>
    有為道作穢？</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>What’s considered the most supreme path,<br/>
    Skillfully teaching the path’s meaning,<br/>
    Getting livelihood from the path,<br/>
    Or being defiled while on the path?</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>能度恩愛刺，<br/>
    入涅槃無疑；<br/>
    超越天人路，<br/>
    說此道殊勝。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>To cross over the thorns of love,<br/>
    Enter <em>nirvāṇa</em> without doubt,<br/>
    And leap over the road of gods and humans,<br/>
    I say this path is the most supreme.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>善解第一義，<br/>
    說道無垢穢；<br/>
    慈仁決眾疑，<br/>
    是為善說道。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Understanding well the supreme meaning,<br/>
    Teaching the path that lacks defilement,<br/>
    And kindly settling myriad doubts,<br/>
    This is skillfully teaching the path.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>善敷演法句，<br/>
    依道以自生；<br/>
    遙望無垢場，<br/>
    名依道生活。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Skillfully elaborating Dharma passages,<br/>
    Basing one’s life on the path,<br/>
    And seeing the immaculate state from afar,<br/>
    This is called getting livelihood from the path.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>內懷於姧邪，<br/>
    外像如清白；<br/>
    虛誑無誠實，<br/>
    此為道作穢。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Being perverted inside one’s heart,<br/>
    Looking pure and clean on the outside,<br/>
    And being fraudulent and dishonest,<br/>
    This is being defiled while on the path.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18b28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>云何善惡俱？<br/>
    淨與不淨雜，<br/>
    相似現外好，<br/>
    如銅為金塗。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>How can one be both good and bad,<br/>
    Mixing the pure with the impure?<br/>
    They appear attractive on the outside<br/>
    Like copper rubbed with gold.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18c1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>俗人遂見此，<br/>
    謂聖智弟子，<br/>
    餘者不盡爾，<br/>
    勿捨清淨信。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Ordinary people who meet them<br/>
    Call them noble and wise disciples.<br/>
    Not all the rest are like that,<br/>
    So they don’t lose their faith.
</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18c3'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>一人持大眾，<br/>
    內濁而外清；<br/>
    現閉姧邪迹，<br/>
    而實懷放蕩。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>A person with a great assembly,<br/>
    Who’s turbid inside and clear outside,<br/>
    Keeps their perversions private,<br/>
    But they’re self-indulgent at heart.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18c5'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>勿視外容貌，<br/>
    卒見便親敬；<br/>
    現閉姧邪迹，<br/>
    而實懷放蕩。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Don’t look at outward appearance,<br/>
    And give your esteem automatically.<br/>
    Some keep their perversions private,<br/>
    But they’re self-indulgent at heart.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c7'>爾時，周那取一小座於佛前坐，佛漸為說法。 示教利喜已，大眾圍遶，侍從而還。</td>
    <td id='p221'>221. Cunda then got a small seat and sat in front of the Buddha, and the Buddha gradually taught him the Dharma. After teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting him, the Buddha returned to the park with the great assembly surrounding him.</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>23. Two Equal Offerings</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c8'>中路止一樹下，告阿難言：「吾患背痛，汝可敷座。」</td>
    <td id='p222'>222. The Buddha stopped under a tree on the road and told Ānanda, “Could you prepare a seat? My back is aching.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c9'>對曰：「唯然！」尋即敷座，世尊止息。時，阿難又敷一小座於佛前坐。</td>
    <td id='p223'>223. Ānanda replied, “Very well,” and he prepared a seat right away so that the Bhagavān could take a break. Ānanda also prepared a small seat for himself and sat in front of the Buddha.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c11'>佛告阿難：「向者周那無悔恨意耶？設有此意，為由何生？」</td>
    <td id='p224'>224. The Buddha asked Ānanda, “Did Cunda have any regrets or resentments about what happened? If he did, what was the reason for it?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c12'>阿難白佛言：「周那設供，無有福利。所以者何？如來最後於其舍食便取涅槃。」</td>
    <td id='p225'>225. Ānanda said to the Buddha, “Cunda provided alms that didn’t bring any benefit. Why is that? The Buddha had the final meal before obtaining <em>nirvāṇa</em> at his home.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c14'>佛告阿難：「勿作是言！勿作是言！今者周那為獲大利，為得壽命，得色，得力，得善名譽，生多財寶，死得生天，所欲自然。所以者何？佛初成道能施食者，佛臨滅度能施食者，此二功德正等無異。汝今可往語彼周那：『我親從佛聞，親受佛教，周那設食，今獲大利，得大果報。』」</td>
    <td id='p226'>226. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Don’t say that. Don’t say that. Cunda will get a great reward in the present. He’ll have a long life both in appearance and strength, and he’ll get a good reputation from it. He’ll create many treasures, and he’ll be born in heaven when he dies. He’ll naturally get what he desires. Why is that? The two virtues of providing a meal to a Buddha when he’s just achieved awakening and when he’s about to be extinguished are perfectly and completely without difference. Now, you can go and tell Cunda, ‘I personally heard and received this from the Buddha: “The meal that Cunda provided will bring a great reward in the present. It will obtain a great result.”’”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.18c20'>時，阿難承佛教旨，即詣彼所，告周那曰： 「我親從佛聞，親從佛受教， 周那設食，今獲大利， 得大果報。 所以然者？ 佛初得道能飯食者，及臨滅度能飯食者，此二功德正等無異。」</td>
    <td id='p227'>227. Ānanda got the point of the Buddha’s instruction and went to Cunda to tell him, “I personally heard and received this from the Buddha: ‘The meal that Cunda provided will bring a great reward in the present. It will obtain a great result. Why is that? The two virtues of providing a meal to a Buddha when he’s just achieved awakening and when he’s about to be extinguished are perfectly and completely without difference.’”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18c26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>周那舍食已，<br/>
    始聞如此言；<br/>
    如來患甚篤，<br/>
    壽行今將訖。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>After the meal at Cunda’s home,<br/>
    Such words were heard for the first time.<br/>
    The Tathāgata’s discomfort was severe,<br/>
    His life and practice coming to an end.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.18c28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>雖食栴檀耳，<br/>
    而患猶更增；<br/>
    抱病而涉路，<br/>
    漸向拘夷城。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Although he only ate sandalwood ears,<br/>
    His discomfort still increased.<br/>
    He embraced it on the road<br/>
    And made his way to Kuśinagara.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a1'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>24. Meeting Pukkaśa</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a1'>爾時，世尊即從座起，小復前行，詣一樹下，又告阿難：「吾背痛甚，汝可敷座。」</td>
    <td id='p228'>228. The Bhagavān then rose from his seat, walked for a while on the road, and then went to a tree again. He said to Ānanda, “This backache is excruciating. Could you prepare a seat for me?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a2'>對曰：「唯然！」尋即敷座，如來止息。阿難禮佛足已，在一面坐。</td>
    <td id='p229'>229. Ānanda replied, “Very well,” and he immediately prepared a seat for the Tathāgata to rest. Ānanda then bowed at the Buddha’s feet and sat to one side.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a4'>時，有阿羅漢弟子，名曰福貴，於拘夷那竭城向波婆城，中路見佛在一樹下，容貌端正，諸根寂定，得上調意第一寂滅。譬如大龍，亦如澄水，清淨無穢，見已歡喜，善心生焉。</td>
    <td id='p230'>230. There was an arhat disciple named Pukkaśa who was headed to Kuśinagara from Pāpā. He saw the Buddha under a tree on the road. He looked handsome and upright with peaceful and settled faculties. He was the most well-behaved [person he’d seen], and his tranquility was supreme. He was like a great nāga in clear water without any dirt [and adorned with the thirty-two signs and eighty excellent features.] He rejoiced upon seeing him, and a good thought arose in him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a8'>即到佛所，頭面禮足，在一面坐，而白佛言：「世尊！出家之人在清淨處，慕樂閑居，甚奇特也。有五百乘車經過其邊，而不聞見，我師一時在拘夷那竭城、波婆城，二城中間道側樹下，靜默而坐，時有五百乘車經過其邊，車聲轟轟覺而不聞。是時，有人來問我師：『向群車過，寧見不耶？』</td>
    <td id='p231'>231. When he reached the Buddha, he bowed his head at his feet and withdrew to sit to one side. He then said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, it’s extraordinary for someone who’s left home to reside in a pure state and love being in a quiet place. Just now, there were 500 carts passing by, but you didn’t hear or see them. There was a time when my teacher was sitting quietly under a tree between Kuśinagara and Pāvā. When 500 carts and chariots passed by him, he didn’t hear the noise and ruckus that they made. Someone came and asked my teacher, ‘Didn’t you see that group of carts pass by?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a14'>對曰：『不見！』</td>
    <td id='p232'>232. “He replied, ‘I didn’t.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a14'>又問：『聞耶？』</td>
    <td id='p233'>233. “They then asked, ‘Did you hear them?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a15'>對曰：『不聞！』</td>
    <td id='p234'>234. “He replied, ‘I didn’t.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a15'>又問：『汝在此耶？在餘處耶？』</td>
    <td id='p235'>235. “They again asked, ‘Were you here or somewhere else?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a16'>答曰：『在此！』</td>
    <td id='p236'>236. “He replied, ‘I was here.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a16'>又問：『汝醒悟耶？』</td>
    <td id='p237'>237. “They asked, ‘Were you awake?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a16'>答曰：『醒悟！』</td>
    <td id='p238'>238. “He replied, ‘I was awake.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a17'>又問：『汝為覺寐？』</td>
    <td id='p239'>239. “They asked, ‘Were you awake or asleep?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a17'>答曰：『不寐！』</td>
    <td id='p240'>240. “He replied, ‘I wasn’t asleep.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a17'>彼人默念：『是希有也！出家之人專精乃爾，車聲轟轟覺而不聞。』</td>
    <td id='p241'>241. “That person thought, ‘That’s amazing! This homeless man was so focused that he didn’t notice or hear the noise and ruckus those carts made!’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a19'>即語我師曰： 『向有五百乘車從此道過，車聲振動，尚自不聞， 豈他聞哉！』 即為作禮，歡喜而去。」</td>
    <td id='p242'>242. “Then that person said to my teacher, ‘The noise of those 500 carts and chariots shook the ground as they passed by on the road, yet you still didn’t hear them. How could you have heard any other sound?’ He then bowed to my teacher, rejoiced, and departed.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a21'>佛告福貴：「我今問汝，隨意所答，群車振動覺而不聞，雷動天地覺而不聞，何者為難？」</td>
    <td id='p243'>243. The Buddha told Pukkaśa, “Now, I will ask you a question. Answer it how you think. Is it harder for someone to be awake when a group of carts shake the ground and not hear it or to be awake and not hear thunder shake heaven and earth?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a23'>福貴白佛言：「千萬車聲，豈等雷電？不聞車聲未足為難，雷動天地覺而不聞，斯乃為難。」</td>
    <td id='p244'>244. Pukkaśa said to the Buddha, “How could the noise of 100,000 carts be equal to that of a thunderclap? It wouldn’t be hard to ignore the noise of the carts, but it’d be a difficult thing to be awake when thunder shakes heaven and earth and not hear it!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19a25'>佛告福貴： 「我於一時遊阿越村，在一草廬。 時有異雲暴起，雷電霹靂。 殺四特牛、耕者兄弟二人。 人眾大聚。時，我出草廬，彷徉經行。 彼大眾中有一人來至我所，頭面禮足，隨我經行。 我知而故問： 『彼大眾聚何所為耶？』</td>
    <td id='p245'>245. The Buddha told Pukkaśa, “I once traveled to the village Ādumā (?) and stayed in a thatched hut. There was an unusually intense downpour with lightning and thunder. Four oxen and two brothers tilling their field were killed. A crowd of people had gathered together when I came out of my grass hut to pace back and forth. Someone from the crowd came over to me, bowed their head at my feet, and followed along as I walked. Knowing the answer, I asked, ‘Why has this large crowd gathered?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b1'>其人即問：『佛向在何所？為覺寐耶？』</td>
    <td id='p246'>246. “That person then asked, ‘Where was the Buddha when it happened? Was he awake or asleep?’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b2'>答曰：『在此！時，不寐也。』</td>
    <td id='p247'>247. “I answered, ‘I was here, and I wasn’t asleep at the time.’</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b2'>其人亦歎希聞得定如佛者也，雷電霹靂，聲聒天地，而獨寂定覺而不聞。</td>
    <td id='p248'>248. “That person praised it as a rare thing to hear of a <em>samādhi</em> like the Buddha’s. The lightning and thunder was a loud noise in heaven and earth, but I was the only awake person who didn’t hear it in my peaceful <em>samādhi</em>.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b4'>乃白佛言：『向有異雲暴起，雷電霹靂，殺四特牛、耕者兄弟二人，彼大眾聚，其正為此。』其人心悅即得法喜，禮佛而去。」</td>
    <td id='p249'>249. “Then he said to the Buddha, ‘There was an unusually intense downpour with lightning and thunder. Four oxen and two brothers tilling their field were killed. That’s what they are doing here.’ Glad and joyful to obtain the Dharma, that person bowed to the Buddha and departed.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b7'>爾時，福貴被二黃疊，價直百千， 即從座起，長跪叉手而白佛言： 「今以此疊奉上世尊， 願垂納受。」</td>
    <td id='p250'>250. That Pukkaśa was at the time wearing two sheets of yellow cloth worth 100,000 [ounces of gold]. He rose from his seat, saluted the Buddha with his palms together, and said to the Buddha, “Now, I offer these sheets of cloth to the Bhagavān. Please accept it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b9'>佛告福貴：「汝以一疊施我，一施阿難。」</td>
    <td id='p251'>251. The Buddha told Pukkaśa, “Give one to me and one to Ānanda.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b10'>爾時，福貴承佛教旨，一奉如來，一施阿難。佛愍彼故，即為納受。</td>
    <td id='p252'>252. Pukkaśa got the point of the Buddha’s instruction and offered one sheet to the Tathāgata and gave one to Ānanda. The Buddha accepted them out of compassion for him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b11'>時，福貴禮佛足已，於一面坐，佛漸為說法，示教利喜：施論、戒論、生天之論，欲為大患、不淨、穢污，上漏為礙，出要為上。</td>
    <td id='p253'>253. After bowing at the Buddha’s feet, Pukkaśa then sat down to one side. The Buddha then gradually taught him the Dharma, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting him. He discussed generosity, precepts, how to be born in heaven, the great danger of desire that’s polluting and impure, and the obstacle of the higher contaminants. [He praised] escaping it as [the most subtle, pure, and] supreme thing.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b14'>時，佛知福貴意，歡喜柔軟，無諸蓋、纏，易可開化，如諸佛常法，即為福貴說苦聖諦，苦集、苦滅、苦出要諦。</td>
    <td id='p254'>254. The Buddha knew Pukkaśa’s mind was joyous and softened, didn’t have hindrances or entanglements, and easily educated. As buddhas always do, the Buddha then taught Pukkaśa the noble truth of suffering … suffering’s formation … suffering’s cessation, and the noble truth of suffering’s escape.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b16'>時，福貴信心清淨，譬如淨潔白疊，易為受色，即於座上遠塵離垢，諸法法眼生，見法得法，決定正住，不墮惡道，成就無畏，而白佛言：「我今歸依佛！歸依法！歸依僧！唯願如來聽我於正法中為優婆塞，自今已後，盡壽不殺、不盜、不婬、不欺、不飲酒，唯願世尊聽我於正法中為優婆塞。」</td>
    <td id='p255'>255. Pukkaśa’s belief was purified. Like a white cloth that readily accepts a dye, he became removed from dust and free of defilement right there on his seat, and the Dharma eye arose in him. He saw the Dharma, got the Dharma, and was certain of the proper abode. He wouldn’t fall to unpleasant destinies, and he achieved fearlessness. He said to the Buddha, “Now, I take refuge in the Buddha, take refuge in the Dharma, and take refuge in the Saṅgha. Please, Tathāgata, permit me to become a layman in the correct teaching. From now on, I won’t kill, steal, commit sexual [misconduct], lie, or drink alcohol for the rest of my life. Please, Bhagavān, permit me to become a layman in the correct teaching!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b23'>又白佛言： 「世尊！遊化若詣波婆城，唯願屈意過貧聚中。 所以然者？ 欲盡家所有飲食、牀臥、衣服、湯藥，奉獻世尊， 世尊受已，家內獲安。」</td>
    <td id='p256'>256. He also said to the Buddha, “Bhagavān, if you happen to visit Pāpā during your teaching tours, please consider visiting our poor district. Why is that? All the households there will have meals, sleeping arrangements, clothing, and medicines to offer the Bhagavān. After he accepts these offerings, those families will find peace.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b26'>佛言：「汝所言善！」</td>
    <td id='p257'>257. The Buddha said, “That sounds good!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b27'>爾時，世尊為福貴說法，示教利喜已。即從座起，頭面禮足，歡喜而去。</td>
    <td id='p258'>258. After the Bhagavān had taught Pukkaśa the Dharma, teaching, instructing, profiting, and delighting him, he rose from his seat, bowed his head at the Buddha’s feet, rejoiced, and departed.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19b28'>其去未久，阿難尋以黃疊奉上如來，如來哀愍，即為受之，被於身上。</td>
    <td id='p259'>259. Not long after he had left, Ānanda offered his sheet of yellow cloth to the Tathāgata, and the Tathāgata accepted it and put it on out of compassion for him.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c1'>爾時，世尊顏貌從容， 威光熾盛，諸根清淨，面色和悅。 阿難見已，默自思念： 「自我得侍二十五年，未曾見佛面色光澤，發明如今。」</td>
    <td id='p260'>260. The Bhagavān’s countenance was relaxed. His majestic glow was brilliant, his faculties were pure, and his face was happy. Seeing this, Ānanda thought to himself, “I’ve been his attendant for twenty-five years, but I’ve never seen the luster of the Buddha’s face be as bright as it is now!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c4'>即從座起，右膝著地，叉手合掌，前白佛言： 「自我得侍二十五年，未曾見佛光色如今， 不審何緣？ 願聞其意！」</td>
    <td id='p261'>261. He rose from his seat, knelt on his right knee, saluted with his palms together, and said to the Buddha, “I’ve been his attendant for twenty-five years, but I’ve never seen the luster of the Buddha’s face be as bright as it is now! It’s unclear to me why that is. I’d like to hear his thoughts.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c6'>佛告阿難：「有二因緣，如來光色有殊於常：一者佛初得道，成無上正真覺時；二者臨欲滅度，捨於性命般涅槃時。阿難，以此二緣，光色殊常。」</td>
    <td id='p262'>262. The Buddha told Ānanda, “There are two circumstances for the Tathāgata’s luster to be more exceptional than usual. First is when the Buddha first attains the path and achieves the unsurpassed and perfect awakening. Second is when a Buddha wants to be extinguished soon and discards his life force for parinirvāṇa. Ānanda, a Tathāgata’s luster is exceptional because of these two circumstances.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c10'>爾時，世尊即說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p263'>263. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c11'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「金色衣光悅，<br/>
    細軟極鮮淨；<br/>
    福貴奉世尊，<br/>
    如雪白毫光。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“His golden robe shined with happiness,<br/>
    Fine, soft, immaculate, and pure.<br/>
    Pukkaśa offered to the Bhagavān<br/>
    Light like his snow white eyebrow tuft.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c13'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>25. The Buddha Asks for Water</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c13'>佛命阿難：「吾渴欲飲，汝取水來。」</td>
    <td id='p265'>265. The Buddha ordered Ānanda, “I’m thirsty; I’d like something to drink. Go and fetch some water.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c13'>阿難白言：「向有五百乘車於上流渡，水濁未清，可以洗足，不中飲也。」</td>
    <td id='p266'>266. Ānanda said, “500 carts recently forded the river upstream. The water is still muddy. We could bathe in it but not drink from it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c15'>如是三勅：「阿難，汝取水來。」</td>
    <td id='p267'>267. The Buddha ordered him three times, “Ānanda, go and fetch some water.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c16'>阿難白言：「今拘孫河去此不遠，清冷可飲，亦可澡浴。」</td>
    <td id='p268'>268. Ānanda said, “It’s not far to go to Kukustā River. It’s clear and cool to drink, and we could bathe in it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.19c17'>時，有鬼神居在雪山，篤信佛道，即以鉢盛八種淨水，奉上世尊。佛愍彼故，尋為受之，而說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p269'>269. There was then a yakṣa spirit residing in the Himalayas that believed in the Buddha’s path. It filled a bowl with water of eight kinds of purity and offered it to the Bhagavān. The Buddha immediately accepted it out of compassion for it. He then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「佛以八種音，<br/>
    勅阿難取水，<br/>
    吾渴今欲飲，<br/>
    飲已詣拘尸；</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“With his eightfold voice, the Buddha<br/>
    Ordered Ānanda to fetch water:<br/>
    ‘I’m thirsty. I want a drink now.<br/>
    After I drink, let’s go to Kuśinagara.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c22'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>柔軟和雅音，<br/>
    所言悅眾心。<br/>
    給侍佛左右，<br/>
    尋白於世尊：</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>With a gentle and peaceful voice,<br/>
    His words made many hearts glad.<br/>
    Serving the Buddha right and left,<br/>
    He quickly said to the Buddha:</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c24'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>向有五百車，<br/>
    截流渡彼岸，<br/>
    渾濁於此水，<br/>
    飲恐不便身；</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>‘Earlier, 500 carts<br/>
    Forded to the other side of the river.<br/>
    They muddied the river’s waters;<br/>
    I fear it’s not suitable to drink.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>拘留河不遠，<br/>
    水美甚清冷；<br/>
    往彼可取飲，<br/>
    亦可澡浴身。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The Kukustā River is not far away;<br/>
    Its water is delicious and refreshing.<br/>
    If we go there, we could get a drink<br/>
    And we could bathe ourselves, too.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.19c28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>雪山有鬼神，<br/>
    奉上如來水；<br/>
    飲已威勢強，<br/>
    眾中師子步。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>A yakṣa spirit in the Himalayas<br/>
    Offered water to the Tathāgata.<br/>
    His majestic strength restored,<br/>
    He paced like a lion in the assembly.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a1'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>其水神龍居，<br/>
    清澄無濁穢；<br/>
    聖顏如雪山，<br/>
    安詳度拘孫。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The river was home to a spirit nāga;<br/>
    It clarified the water of its muddiness.<br/>
    His noble appearance like the Himalayas,<br/>
    The Buddha calmly crossed the Kukustā.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a3'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>26. Cunda’s Parinirvāṇa</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a3'>爾時，世尊即詣拘孫河。 飲已澡浴，與眾而去。 中路止息在一樹下，告周那曰： 「汝取僧伽梨四牒而敷。 吾患背痛，欲暫止息。」</td>
    <td id='p271'>271. The Bhagavān then went to Kukustā River. After drinking, he bathed and then departed with the assembly. They stopped to rest under a tree on the road, and he told Cunda, “Take an outer robe, fold it four times, and lay it out. My back is hurting, and I want to rest for a while.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a5'>周那受教，敷置已訖，佛坐其上。周那禮已，於一面坐，而白佛言：「我欲般涅槃！我欲般涅槃！」</td>
    <td id='p272'>272. Cunda accepted this instruction. He laid out the robe, and the Buddha sat on it. Cunda then bowed to him and sat to one side. He said to the Buddha, “I want to parinirvāṇa! I want to parinirvāṇa!”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a8'>佛告之曰：「宜知是時。」於是，周那即於佛前便般涅槃，</td>
    <td id='p273'>273. The Buddha told him, “It’s up to you to decide when it’s time.” Cunda thereupon parinirvāṇa-ed right there in front of the Buddha.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a9'>佛時頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p274'>274. The Buddha then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a10'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「佛趣拘孫河，<br/>
    清涼無濁穢，<br/>
    人中尊入水，<br/>
    澡浴度彼岸。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The Buddha headed for Kukustā River,<br/>
    Cool, refreshing, and without muddiness.<br/>
    The sage among humans entered the water,<br/>
    Bathed, and crossed to the other side.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a12'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>大眾之原首，<br/>
    教勅於周那：<br/>
    吾今身疲極，<br/>
    汝速敷臥具；</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>The founder of the great assembly<br/>
    Gave his instruction to Cunda:<br/>
    ‘My body is worn out now.<br/>
    Quickly, lay out a bed for me.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a14'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>周那尋受教，<br/>
    四牒衣而敷；<br/>
    如來既止息，<br/>
    周那於前坐。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Cunda accepted it at once,<br/>
    Folding a robe and laying it out.<br/>
    The Tathāgata then rested there<br/>
    With Cunda sitting in front of him.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a16'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>即白於世尊：<br/>
    我欲取滅度，<br/>
    無愛無憎處，<br/>
    今當到彼方。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>He said to the Bhagavān,<br/>
    ‘I want to obtain extinguishment.<br/>
    That place without love or hate,<br/>
    That’s where I will arrive now.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a18'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>無量功德海，<br/>
    最勝告彼曰：<br/>
    汝所作已辦，<br/>
    今宜知是時。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>That sea of measureless virtue,<br/>
    The Supreme One told him,<br/>
    ‘You have accomplished the task;<br/>
    It’s up to you to decide when it’s time.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20a20'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>見佛已聽許，<br/>
    周那倍精勤；<br/>
    滅行無有餘，<br/>
    如燈盡火滅。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>After recognizing the Buddha’s assent,<br/>
    Cunda then redoubled his efforts.<br/>
    Volition ceased without remainder,<br/>
    Like a lamp’s flame going out completely.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a22'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>27. The Funeral of a Buddha</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a22'>時，阿難即從座起，前白佛言：「佛滅度後，葬法云何？」</td>
    <td id='p275'>275. Ānanda then rose from his seat and went before the Buddha. He said, “After a buddha is extinguished, how is he interred?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a23'>佛告阿難：「汝且默然，思汝所業，諸清信士自樂為之。」</td>
    <td id='p276'>276. The Buddha told Ānanda, “You, be quiet and think about your actions. The faithful laymen would be happy to do it.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a24'>時，阿難復重三啟：「佛滅度後，葬法云何？」</td>
    <td id='p277'>277. Ānanda repeated his inquiry three times: “After a buddha is extinguished, how is he interred?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a25'>佛言：「欲知葬法者，當如轉輪聖王。」</td>
    <td id='p278'>278. The Buddha said, “Someone wanting to know the way to inter a buddha should do it the way they would a noble wheel-turning king.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a26'>阿難又白：「轉輪聖王葬法云何？」</td>
    <td id='p279'>279. Ānanda then asked, “What’s the way a noble wheel-turning king is interred?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20a26'>佛告阿難：「聖王葬法，先以香湯洗浴其體，以新劫貝周遍纏身，以五百張疊次如纏之。內身金棺灌以麻油畢，舉金棺置於第二大鐵槨中，栴檀香槨次重於外，積眾名香，厚衣其上而闍維之。訖收舍利，於四衢道起立塔廟，表剎懸繒，使國行人皆見法王塔，思慕正化，多所饒益。</td>
    <td id='p280'>280. The Buddha told Ānanda, “This is the way a noble king is interred. First, his body is bathed in fragrant water. He’s wrapped all around with fresh cotton. Next, he’s wrapped in 500 layers of cloth. His body is placed in a gold coffin and sesame oil is poured onto it. The gold coffin is lifted and placed in a second larger iron coffin. Sandalwood incense is next layered outside of that coffin. Firewood of many fragrances is piled on top of fine robes, and then he is cremated. When it’s done, his remains are placed in a shrine built at a crossroads. It’s exterior is hung with silks, and people from the country travel to see the Dharma King’s shrine. They think longingly about the correct teaching that benefited many people.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20b4'>阿難，汝欲葬我，先以香湯洗浴，用新劫貝周遍纏身，以五百張疊次如纏之。內身金棺灌以麻油畢，舉金棺置於第二大鐵槨中，旃檀香槨次重於外，積眾名香，厚衣其上而闍維之。訖收舍利，於四衢道起立塔廟，表剎懸繒，使諸行人皆見佛塔，思慕如來法王道化，生獲福利，死得上天。」</td>
    <td id='p281'>281. “Ānanda, when you inter me, first bathe my body in fragrant water and then wrap it all around with fresh cotton. Wrap it in 500 layers of cloth, place my body inside a gold coffin, and pour sesame oil on it. Lift the gold coffin and place it in a second larger iron coffin. Next, layer sandalwood incense on the outside of that coffin. Pile firewood of many fragrances on top of fine robes, and then cremate me. When it’s done, place my remains in a shrine built at a crossroads. Hang its exterior with silks, and have people from the country travel to see the Buddha’s shrine. They’ll think longingly about the Tathāgata, the Dharma King, and his awakened teaching. While they’re alive, they will get merits and rewards, and they’ll be born up in heaven when they die.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20b11'>於時，世尊重觀此義，而說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p282'>282. Thereupon, the Bhagavān restated this by speaking in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b13'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「阿難從坐起，<br/>
    長跪白世尊，<br/>
    如來滅度後，<br/>
    當以何法葬？</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“Ānanda rose from his seat<br/>
    And said to the Bhagavān while kneeling:<br/>
    ‘After the Tathāgata is extinguished,<br/>
    What’s the way he should be interred?’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b15'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>阿難汝且默，<br/>
    思惟汝所行；<br/>
    國內諸清信，<br/>
    自當樂為之。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>‘Ānanda, you stay quiet<br/>
    And think about your actions.<br/>
    The laypeople in this country<br/>
    Will be happy to do it.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b17'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>阿難三請已，<br/>
    佛說轉輪葬，<br/>
    欲葬如來身，<br/>
    疊裹內棺槨；</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>After Ānanda asked this thrice,<br/>
    The Buddha described interring a noble king.<br/>
    ‘When you inter the Tathāgata’s body,<br/>
    Wrap it in cloth and put it in two coffins.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b19'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>四衢起塔廟，<br/>
    為利益眾生，<br/>
    諸有禮敬者，<br/>
    皆獲無量福。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Build a shrine at a crossroads<br/>
    For the benefit of sentient beings.<br/>
    Those who come to venerate it<br/>
    Will obtain measureless merit.’”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20b21'>佛告阿難： 「天下有四種人，應得起塔，香花繒蓋，伎樂供養。 何等為四？ 一者如來應得起塔，二者辟支佛，三者聲聞人，四者轉輪王。阿難，此四種人應得起塔，香華繒蓋，伎樂供養。」</td>
    <td id='p283'>283. The Buddha told Ānanda, “There are four kinds of people in the world who should be memorialized by building a shrine and providing incense, flowers, silk canopies, and music there. Who are the four? First, a tathāgata should have a shrine built for him. Second is a pratyeka buddha. Third is a disciple [of a buddha]. Fourth is a [noble] wheel-turning king. Ānanda, these four kinds of people should be memorialized by building a shrine and providing incense, flowers, silk canopies, and music.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20b25'>爾時，世尊以偈頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p284'>284. The Bhagavān then spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b26'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「佛應第一塔，<br/>
    辟支佛聲聞，<br/>
    及轉輪聖王，<br/>
    典領四域主。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“A buddha’s shrine is first,<br/>
    Then a pratyeka buddha’s, disciple’s,<br/>
    And noble wheel-turning king’s,<br/>
    Who ruled over the four regions.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20b28'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>斯四應供養，<br/>
    如來之所記，<br/>
    佛、辟支、聲聞，<br/>
    及轉輪王塔。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>These four deserve memorials<br/>
    As the Tathāgata has described,<br/>
    At a buddha’s, pratyeka buddha’s, disciple’s,<br/>
    And a noble wheel-turning king’s shrine.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c1'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>28. An Ascetic’s Offering</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c1'>爾時，世尊告阿難：「俱詣拘尸城，末羅雙樹間。」</td>
    <td id='p285'>285. The Bhagavān then told Ānanda, “Let’s go to Kuśinagara [and stop] between a pair of trees in Malla.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c2'>對曰：「唯然！」 即與大眾圍遶世尊，在道而行。</td>
    <td id='p286'>286. Ānanda replied, “Very well.” The Buddha was surrounded by the great assembly as he walked on the road.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c3'>有一梵志從拘尸城趣波婆城，中路遙見世尊顏貌端正，諸根寂定，見已歡喜，善心自生，前至佛所，問訊訖，一面住，而白佛言：「我所居村去此不遠，唯願瞿曇於彼止宿，清旦食已，然後趣城。」</td>
    <td id='p287'>287. There was an ascetic from Kuśinagara headed to Pāvā who saw the Bhagavān on the road from afar. He looked handsome and upright with peaceful and settled faculties. The ascetic rejoiced upon seeing him, and a good thought arose in him. He went before the Buddha, exchanged greetings with him, and stood to one side. He said to the Buddha, “The village where I live is not far to go. Please stop there, Gautama. After having a meal in the morning, you could continue on to your destination.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c7'>佛告梵志：「且止！且止！汝今便為供養我已。」</td>
    <td id='p288'>288. The Buddha told the ascetic, “Stop! Stop! You’ve already made an offering to me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c8'>時，梵志慇懃三請，佛答如初，又告梵志：「阿難在後，汝可語意。」</td>
    <td id='p289'>289. The ascetic persisted in inviting him three times, and the Buddha’s answer was the same as the first time. He then told the ascetic, “Ānanda will be here later; you can tell him about your wishes then.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c9'>時，梵志聞佛教已，即詣阿難，問訊已，於一面立，白阿難言：「我所居村去此不遠，欲屈瞿曇於彼止宿，清旦食已，然後趣城。」</td>
    <td id='p290'>290. After hearing the Buddha’s instruction, the ascetic went to Ānanda. After exchanging greetings, he stood to one side. He said, “The village where I live is not far to go. I’d like Gautama to consider stopping there. After having a meal in the morning, he could continue on to his destination.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c12'>阿難報曰： 「止！止！梵志！汝今已為得供養已。」</td>
    <td id='p291'>291. Ānanda replied, “Stop, stop! Ascetic, you’ve already given offerings to him.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c13'>梵志復請，慇懃至三，阿難答曰：「時既暑熱，彼村遠逈，世尊疲極，不足勞嬈。」</td>
    <td id='p292'>292. The ascetic still persisted with his invitation three times. Ānanda replied, “The weather is too hot right now, and your village is too far to go. The Bhagavān is exhausted and not strong enough.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.20c15'>爾時，世尊觀此義已，即說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p293'>293. After contemplating the meaning of this, the Bhagavān spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c17'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「淨眼前進路，<br/>
    疲極向雙樹；<br/>
    梵志遙見佛，<br/>
    速詣而𥡳首，</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The Pure Eye made progress on the road;<br/>
    Exhausted, he stopped between a pair of trees.<br/>
    An ascetic saw the Buddha from a distance,<br/>
    Hurried over to him, and bowed his head.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c19'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>我村今在近，<br/>
    哀愍留一宿；<br/>
    清旦設微供，<br/>
    然後向彼城。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>‘My village is nearby now,<br/>
    Have pity and stay there for a night.<br/>
    I’ll provide alms in the morning;<br/>
    Afterward, you can continue to that city.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c21'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>梵志我身倦，<br/>
    道遠不能過；<br/>
    監藏者在後，<br/>
    汝可住語意。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>‘Ascetic, my body is worn out.<br/>
    It’s too far to go, beyond what I can do.<br/>
    The attendant is sitting over there;<br/>
    You can tell him about your wishes.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c23'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>承佛教旨已，<br/>
    即詣阿難所：<br/>
    唯願至我村，<br/>
    清旦食已去。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Getting the Buddha’s point,<br/>
    He went to Ānanda:<br/>
    ‘Please go to my village.<br/>
    You can depart after the morning meal.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c25'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>阿難曰止止，<br/>
    時熱不相赴。<br/>
    三請不遂願，<br/>
    憂惱不悅樂。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Ānanda said, ‘Stop, stop!<br/>
    It’s too hot, he can’t go so far.’<br/>
    Three invitations weren’t accepted,<br/>
    He was dejected and unhappy.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c27'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>咄此有為法，<br/>
    流遷不常住；<br/>
    今於雙樹間，<br/>
    滅我無漏身。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Oh, how this conditioned state<br/>
    Flows, changes, and isn’t constant!<br/>
    Here, between a pair of trees,<br/>
    My uncontaminated body will pass away.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.20c29'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>佛、辟支、聲聞，<br/>
    一切皆歸滅；<br/>
    無常無撰擇，<br/>
    如火焚山林。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Buddhas, pratyeka buddhas, and disciples<br/>
    All return to that same cessation.<br/>
    They have no choice but be impermanent;<br/>
    They’re like mountain forests on fire.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a2'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>29. A Bed Between Two Trees</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a2'>爾時，世尊入拘尸城，向本生處末羅雙樹間， 告阿難曰： 「汝為如來於雙樹間敷置牀座， 使頭北首，面向西方。 所以然者？ 吾法流布，當久住北方。」</td>
    <td id='p294'>294. The Bhagavān arrived in his homeland on the way to Kuśinagara and was between a pair of trees in Malla. He told Ānanda, “Prepare a bed here between this pair of trees. Arrange it so my head is to the north and I’m facing the west. Why is that? My Dharma will spread and remain for a long time in the north.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a5'>對曰：「唯然！」 即敷座，令北首。</td>
    <td id='p295'>295. Ānanda replied, “Very well!” He prepared the bed so the Buddha’s head was to the north.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a6'>爾時，世尊自四牒僧伽梨，偃右脇如師子王，累足而臥。</td>
    <td id='p296'>296. The Bhagavān folded his outer robe four times and laid down on his right side like a lion king, placing one foot on the other.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a7'>時，雙樹間所有鬼神篤信佛者，以非時花布散于地。爾時，世尊告阿難曰：「此雙樹神以非時華供養於我，此非供養如來。」</td>
    <td id='p297'>297. A yakṣa spirit was present between those two trees who believed in the Buddha. It scattered out-of-season flowers on the ground. The Bhagavān told Ānanda, “The spirit of these two trees has offered these out-of-season flowers to me. These are not offerings to the Tathāgata.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a10'>阿難白言：「云何名為供養如來？」</td>
    <td id='p298'>298. Ānanda said, “What would be called an offering to the Tathāgata?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a11'> <!-- [5] -->佛語阿難： 「人能受法，能行法者，斯乃名曰供養如來。」</td>
    <td id='p299'>299. The Buddha said to Ānanda, “When someone can accept the teaching and practice the teaching, I call that an offering to the Tathāgata.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a12'>佛觀此義，而說頌曰：</td>
    <td id='p300'>300. After contemplating this meaning, the Buddha spoke in verse:</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.21a13'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>「佛在雙樹間，<br/>
    偃臥心不亂；<br/>
    樹神心清淨，<br/>
    以花散佛上。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>“The Buddha was between a pair of trees;<br/>
    Lying there, his heart was undisturbed.<br/>
    A tree spirit with a heart that was pure<br/>
    Scattered flowers on the Buddha.</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.21a15'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>阿難白佛言：<br/>
    云何名供養？<br/>
    受法而能行，<br/>
    覺華而為供。</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Ānanda said to the Buddha,<br/>
    ‘What would be called an offering?’<br/>
    ‘Accepting and practicing the teaching;<br/>
    The flower of awakening is an offering.’</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td title='t1.1.21a17'><ul class='verse'>
    <li class='ch'>紫金華如輪，<br/>
    散佛未為供；<br/>
    陰、界、入無我，<br/>
    乃名第一供。」</li>
  </ul></td>
  <td><ul class='verse'>
    <li>Purple gold flowers like cartwheels<br/>
    Had never been scattered on the Buddha.<br/>
    Selfless aggregates, elements, and senses,<br/>
    That’s what’s called the supreme offering.”</li>
  </ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a19'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>30. Upamāna and the Spirits</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a19'>爾時，梵摩那在於佛前執扇扇佛，佛言：「汝却，勿在吾前。」</td>
    <td id='p301'>301. Upamāna was in front of the Buddha fanning him at the time. The Buddha said, “You can retire; don’t stand in front of me.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a20'>時，阿難默自思念： 「此梵摩那常在佛左右，供給所須。 當尊敬如來，視無厭足。 今者末後須其瞻視，乃命使却。 意將何因？」</td>
    <td id='p302'>302. Ānanda then thought to himself, “This Upamāna has always served the Buddha right and left, providing what he needs. He would venerate the Tathāgata and gaze at him without tire. Now, he’s looking after his needs for the final time, but he’s ordered to retire. What was the cause of this?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a23'>於是，阿難即整衣服，前白佛言：「此梵摩那常在佛左右，供給所須，當尊敬如來，視無厭足。今者末後須其瞻視，而命使却，將有何因？」</td>
    <td id='p303'>303. Ānanda then adjusted his robe and went before the Buddha. He said, “This Upamāna has always served the Buddha right and left, providing what he needs. He would venerate the Tathāgata and gaze on him without tire. Now, he’s looking after the Buddha’s needs for the final time, but he was ordered to retire. What was the cause of this?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21a26'>佛告阿難： 「此拘尸城外有十二由旬，皆是諸大神天之所居宅，無空缺處。 此諸大神皆嫌此比丘當佛前立： 『今佛末後垂當滅度，吾等諸神，冀一奉覲，而此比丘有大威德，光明暎蔽， 使我曹等不得親近禮拜供養。』 阿難，我以是緣，故命使却。」</td>
    <td id='p304'>304. The Buddha told Ānanda, “For as far as twelve yojanas outside Kuśinagara, there are great spirits and gods that live here; there’s no space empty of them. Those great spirits were criticizing this monk who was standing in front of the Buddha. ‘Now, finally the Buddha is about to be extinguished, and we spirits have come to present offerings, but this monk is outshining us with his great majesty and glowing light. He’s preventing us from approaching, venerating, and making offerings to the Buddha!’ Ānanda, this is the reason I ordered him to retire.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b2'>阿難白佛： 「此尊比丘本積何德，修何行業，今者威德乃如是乎？」</td>
    <td id='p305'>305. Ānanda said to the Buddha, “What virtue has this venerable monk accumulated and what actions has he cultivated to have such majesty today?”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b4'>佛告阿難： 「乃往過去久遠九十一劫，時世有佛，名毗婆尸， 時此比丘以歡喜心，手執草炬，以照彼塔， 由此因緣，使今威光上徹二十八天， 諸天神光所不能及。」</td>
    <td id='p306'>306. The Buddha told Ānanda, “Long ago, ninety-one eons in the past, there was a Buddha named Vipaśyin. At the time, this monk carried a grass torch with a joyous heart to illuminate his shrine. He has this glowing majesty today that penetrates the twenty-eight heavens above because of this history. The glow of these gods and spirits doesn’t compare to his.”</td>
  </tr>
<tr>
  <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b8'></td>
  <td class='subheading'>31. The Past Glory of Kuśinagara during the Time of Mahāsudarśana</td>
</tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b8'>爾時，阿難即從座起，偏袒右肩，長跪叉手而白佛言： 「莫於此鄙陋小城荒毀之土取滅度也。 所以者何？ 更有大國——瞻婆大國、毗舍離國、王舍城、婆祇國、舍衛國、迦維羅衛國、波羅㮈國，其土人民眾多，信樂佛法。 佛滅度已，必能恭敬供養舍利。」</td>
    <td id='p307'>307. Ānanda then rose from his seat, bared his right shoulder, and saluted with his palms together. He said to the Buddha, “Don’t be extinguished in this inferior, little city, in this barbaric land. Why is that? There are other great countries such as Campā, Vaiśālī, Rājagṛha, Vṛji, Śrāvastī, Kapilavastu, and Bārāṇasī. There are many people among their populations who confidently believe the Buddha’s teaching. After the Buddha’s extinguishment, they surely will pay respects and give offerings to his remains.”</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b13'>佛言：「止！止！勿造斯觀，無謂此土以為鄙陋。所以者何？昔者，此國有王名大善見，</td>
    <td id='p308'>308. The Buddha said, “Stop! Stop! Don’t make such observations. No one takes this land to be barbaric. Why is that? In the past, this country had a king named Mahāsudarśana.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b15'>此城時名拘舍婆提， 大王之都城，長四百八十里，廣二百八十里。 是時，穀米豐賤，人民熾盛， 其城七重，遶城欄楯亦復七重，彫文刻鏤，間懸寶鈴。 其城下基深三仞，高十二仞， 城上樓觀高十二仞，柱圍三仞， 金城銀門，銀城金門； 琉璃城水精門，水精城琉璃門。</td>
    <td id='p309'>309. “At that time, the city here was called Kuśavātī. That Great King’s capital city was 480 yojanas long and 280 yojanas wide. Grain was bountiful there, and its people were prosperous. The city had seven walls, and it was surrounded by seven balustrades that were carved, engraved, and hung with precious bells here and there. The city’s foundation was twenty-four feet deep, and it was 160 feet tall. Its lookout towers rose 160 feet above the city, and there were pillars that were twenty-four feet around. Where the city wall was gold, the gates were silver. The silver city walls had gold gates, the beryl city walls had crystal gates, and the crystal city walls had beryl gates.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b21'>「其城周圓四寶莊嚴， 間錯欄楯亦以四寶。 金樓銀鈴，銀樓金鈴， 寶壍七重，中生蓮花：優鉢羅花、鉢頭摩花、俱物頭花、分陀利花。 下有金沙布現其底， 俠道兩邊生多隣娑樹， 其金樹者，銀葉花實； 其銀樹者，金葉花實；水精樹者，琉璃花實；琉璃樹者，水精花實。</td>
    <td id='p310'>310. “The perimeter of the city was also decorated with the four treasures. The balustrades alternated between the four treasures, too. There were gold towers with silver bells and silver towers with gold bells. It had a sevenfold treasure moat with lotus flowers growing in it, such as utpala flowers, padma flowers, kumuda flowers, and puṇḍarīka flowers. Its bottom was strewn with gold sand. Tāla trees grew on both sides of the road. Some had golden trunks with silver leaves, flowers, and fruit. Others had silver trunks with gold leaves, flowers, and fruit, crystal trunks with beryl leaves, flowers, and fruit, and beryl trunks with crystal leaves, flowers, and fruit.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21b27'>多隣樹間有眾浴池，清流深潭，潔淨無穢， 以四寶塼間砌其邊。 金梯銀蹬，銀梯金蹬，琉璃梯金蹬，琉璃梯陛水精為蹬，水精梯陛琉璃為蹬。 周匝欄楯，遼遶相承，</td>
    <td id='p311'>311. “There were many bathing pools between the tāla trees that were clear, flowing, deep, clean, and unpolluted. They had stairs along their sides with bricks made of the four treasures. The gold stairs had silver steps, the silver stairs had gold steps, the beryl stairs had crystal steps, and the crystal stairs had beryl steps. Balustrades also encircled them in connected circles a distance away [from the pools].</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21c2'>其城處處生多隣樹， 其金樹者，銀葉花實， 其銀樹者，金葉花實， 水精樹者，琉璃花實， 琉璃樹者，水精花實。 樹間亦有四種寶池，生四種花。 街巷齊整，行伍相當， 風吹眾花，紛紛路側。 微風四起，吹諸寶樹，出柔軟音，猶如天樂。 其國人民，男女大小，共遊樹間，以自娛樂。</td>
    <td id='p312'>312. “Tāla trees grew everywhere in the city. Those with gold trunks had silver leaves, flowers, and fruit. Those with silver trunks had gold leaves, flowers, and fruit. Those with crystal trunks and beryl leaves, flowers, and fruit. Those with beryl trunks had crystal leaves, flowers, and fruit. There were also pools made of the four treasures between the trees where the four kinds of flowers grew. The streets were orderly, arranged in grids of five [buildings to a side]. The breeze blew the myriad flowers and scattered them along the roadsides. Light winds blew in all directions through the treasure trees, making gentle sounds like heavenly music. The people of that country, male and female, adults and children, walked among the trees to entertain themselves.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21c8'>其國常有十種聲：貝聲、鼓聲、波羅聲、歌聲、舞聲、吹聲、象聲、馬聲、車聲、飲食戲笑聲。</td>
    <td id='p313'>313. “Ten kinds of sound were constantly heard in that country: Conch sounds, drum sounds, small drum sounds, sounds of singing, sounds of dancing, sounds of flutes, sounds of elephants, sounds of horses, sounds of carts, and the sounds of eating, drinking, and making merry.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21c10'>「爾時，大善見王七寶具足。 王有四德，主四天下。 何謂七寶？ 一、金輪寶。 二、白象寶。 三、紺馬寶。 四、神珠寶。 五、玉女寶。 六、居士寶。 七、主兵寶。</td>
    <td id='p314'>314. “King Mahāsudarśana had possession of the seven treasures then. The King had four virtues and ruled the four continents. What were the seven treasures? First is the golden wheel treasure. Second is the white elephant treasure. Third is the dark blue horse treasure. Fourth is the magic jewel treasure. Fifth is the beautiful woman treasure. Sixth is the householder treasure. Seventh is the army general treasure.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td class='ch' title='t1.1.21c13'>云何善見大王成就金輪寶？王常以十五日月滿時，沐浴香湯，昇高殿上，婇女圍遶，自然輪寶忽現在前，輪有千輻，光色具足，天匠所造，非世所有，真金所成，輪徑丈四。</td>
    <td id='p315'>315. “How did that Great King Mahāsudarśana achieve the golden wheel treasure? Every fifteenth-day full moon, the King bathed in fragrant water, went up into the high hall, and surrounded himself with maidens. The wheel then spontaneously appeared before him. It had a thousand spokes and possessed a glow. It was something made by a heavenly artisan, not something made in this world. It was made of pure gold, and it was about fourteen feet in diameter.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
